" data-portal-copyright= " Peter Power " **28;6142;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77341866 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; HAMILTON &amp;mdash ; It is the biggest splash to hit Hamilton in recent memory , but the Johnny Football Show is still very much in rehearsals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the third day of Tiger-Cats training camp , and four days after the team announced that it had finally landed Johnny Manziel , the would-be NFL star who long ago instead became embattled former quarterback Johnny Manziel , the man himself continued his baby-steps return to football . He took part in some seven-on-seven drills . He made some throws . He continued to study a playbook that even his coach says is &amp;ldquo ; like learning Chinese . &amp;rdquo ; Plus , big field , three downs , 12 players per side , the rouge : there is much to figure out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re piecing it together , &amp;rdquo ; Manziel said after practice at McMaster University . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ll keep doing more and more stuff every single day . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; About the only thing that is already in mid-season form is his media game . Manziel has been doing radio interviews in bunches since he signed on Saturday , and though the clutch of reporters and cameras in Hamilton on Tuesday was small , it was still much larger than would normally be around the team in mid-May . ESPN is expected to have a crew arrive on Wednesday . USA Today will have someone here before the first pre-season game . And then , if Manziel actually plays , look out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It could be a complete disaster and an embarrassment for the CFL . It could be a resounding success that wins the league valuable exposure in the wider football world . It could be both a success and an embarrassment , if Manziel performs well and then leaves at the first opportunity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whatever happens , it won&amp;rsquo ; t go down in the background of a routine season on the south shore of Lake Ontario . Manziel&amp;rsquo ; s arrival has already ensured that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even the most casual of football fans is at least vaguely aware of Manziel&amp;rsquo ; s resume . A high-school star in Texas , he became the starter at Texas A&amp;amp ; M as a freshman in 2012 &amp;mdash ; after , in a bit of foreshadowing , he narrowly avoided discipline for a drunken fight near campus &amp;mdash ; and went on to set a pile of records and win the Heisman Trophy . That season included a win over No. 1-ranked Alabama on the road and back-to-back games in which Manziel amassed more than 500 yards of total offence . His numbers in those 13 games were outrageous : more than 5,000 combined passing and rushing yards , and 47 combined touchdowns against nine interceptions . He was also improvisational and great fun to watch . At 20 years old , Manziel was already a legend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He was also , as is now evident , a bit of a mess . He would remain one more year at school before declaring for the NFL Draft , and was taken in the first round by the Cleveland Browns in 2014 . He would show sparks of his football gifts over two seasons , but had a seemingly well-earned reputation for partying and alcohol over practice and playbooks , and was released before the 2016 season . Charges stemming from a domestic-assault incident that year against a former girlfriend &amp;mdash ; later dropped in plea arrangement &amp;mdash ; followed , and he was dropped by multiple agents and sponsors , with even his father telling a reporter that he feared his son would be dead if he didn&amp;rsquo ; t get the proper help . Manziel , in announcing a planned comeback on Good Morning America this past February , said he had been on medication for bipolar disorder . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since his arrival in Hamilton , Manziel has said that he is not proud of his history &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ll never be able to outrun my past , &amp;rdquo ; he said on Sunday &amp;mdash ; but that it has forced him to grow out of his rich-kid , star-athlete immaturity. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I had no choice but to try to fight my way out of it a little bit , and I&amp;rsquo ; m thankful for the people I have around in my life these days that truly care what I&amp;rsquo ; m doing and what I&amp;rsquo ; m trying to get back to doing , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What he&amp;rsquo ; s trying to do in the short term is win a job , which , despite his talent , is no sure thing . The incumbent Tiger-Cats starter , Jeremiah Masoli , flourished under new head coach June Jones last season , averaging 348 yards passing over Hamilton&amp;rsquo ; s final five games of a lost season . But Jones , who has already suggested Manziel could be an all-time CFL great , did not bring him here so he could hone his clipboard-holding skills . Manziel , though he has said he just wants to see how this comeback goes before thinking about his long-term plans , let slip on Tuesday that he has one path in mind , in response to a question he was asked about Doug Flutie. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t think the CFL is exactly everyone&amp;rsquo ; s end game , for some people it definitely is , but guys are striving to get to the NFL and Doug did that , &amp;rdquo ; Manziel said . &amp;ldquo ; Guys like Warren Moon did that , they played up here at a very high level and then went to the NFL and had success . We had good talks about it , &amp;rdquo ; he said , referring to Flutie. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Did he think , with his running ability , he could have a Flutie-type impact up here ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Right now I&amp;rsquo ; m struggling to see how many guys are on the field and what the defence is doing , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ll cross that bridge when we come to it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He finished his media scrum and walked off . A Ticats official reminded him of a radio interview he was supposed to do later that evening . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That part , at least , is familiar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; &lt;em&gt;Email : &lt;a href= " **29;6172;TOOLONG " **36;6203;TOOLONG Twitter : &lt;a href= " https : **26;6241;TOOLONG " class= " twitter-follow-button " **38;6269;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Scott StinsonTue , 22 May 2018 22:13:20 **45;6309;TOOLONG ... policy at the top of the agenda for this week 's NFL owners ' gatheringhttp : **31;6356;TOOLONG ... src= " https : **33;6389;TOOLONG ... title= " Members of the Houston Texans kneel during the national anthem before an October 2017 game in Seattle . " alt= " Members of the Houston Texans kneel during the national anthem before an October 2017 game in Seattle . " data-portal-copyright= " Jonathan Ferrey " **28;6424;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77340712 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; ATLANTA &amp;mdash ; As NFL owners gather for what promises to be a busy two-day meeting at an Atlanta hotel beginning Tuesday , there remained no clear consensus among them about what to do about the league&amp;rsquo ; s national anthem policy for the 2018 season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There remains a belief that the ultimate resolution will be a compromise under which the policy will be decided on a team-by-team basis , whereby some owners would require players to stand while most probably would not . But the issue remains up for debate and other resolutions still are possible , according to people familiar with the league&amp;rsquo ; s inner workings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Elsewhere , the owners will consider proposed changes to kickoffs and likely will ratify the use of instant replay to aid in decisions about whether to eject a player from a game under the helmet-hitting rule approved in March . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They could also approve the sale of the Carolina Panthers from Jerry Richardson to hedge fund billionaire David Tepper for approximately $2.2 billion , consider the implications of the Supreme Court ruling clearing the way for states to legalize sports gambling , and will decide future sites of Super Bowls and NFL drafts. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But reaching a resolution on the anthem policy would be the most significant development at this meeting , if the owners indeed can reach one . A high-ranking official with one NFL franchise said in recent days he wasn&amp;rsquo ; t certain what it will be , but he continues to believe that a team-by-team decision is most likely . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Houston Texans owner Robert McNair said in March at the annual league meeting that NFL playing fields are not the place for political statements . Dallas Cowboys owner Jerry Jones said last season that he would bench any player on his team who refused to stand for the anthem . Cincinnati Bengals owner Mike Brown reportedly told free agent safety Eric Reid that he plans to require his players to stand for the anthem during the upcoming 2018 season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But other owners have said they do not favour requiring players to stand . The owners declined to enact such a requirement when they met last October in New York . NFL commissioner Roger Goodell and some owners said then that they wanted players to stand for the anthem but weren&amp;rsquo ; t prepared to require it . They said they were focused on discussions with players that led to last year&amp;rsquo ; s social justice initiative in which the league and teams are providing funding for players&amp;rsquo ; community activism. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The owners could opt to leave the current policy unchanged . They could return to the league&amp;rsquo ; s pre-2009 approach of keeping players in the locker rooms until after the anthem is played , or require players on the field to stand while giving them the option to remain in the locker room if they choose . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It does not appear that there is sufficient support among the owners for a leaguewide directive requiring all players to stand . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The current rule , included in the game operations manual sent by the league to teams , requires players to be on the sideline for the anthem . It says that players should stand but it does not require it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The kickoff proposal will be put in front of the owners by the competition committee . It represents what amounts to a last-ditch effort to make the play safer before the league would consider eliminating it entirely . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The proposal , constructed with the input of special teams coaches from around the league , is an attempt to make the kickoff more like a punt , with blockers running alongside potential tacklers down the field instead of meeting them head-on at high speeds . The idea is to prevent would-be tacklers from getting a running start before the ball is kicked and would require eight of the 11 players on the receiving team to line up within 15 yards of the restraining line between the teams . This would create an initial no-hitting zone between the teams and eliminate all forms of &amp;ldquo ; wedge&amp;rdquo ; blocking by two or more players standing side by side . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The proposal must be ratified by at least 24 of the 32 teams . So , too , must the inclusion of replay in the process by which a player would be ejected under the new safety rule approved by the owners in March . That rule makes it a penalty for a player to lower his head and use his helmet to initiate contact with an opponent . The replay element is the final part of that rule that must be considered by the owners , and would make all ejections subject to replay scrutiny . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Owners likely will approve the sale of the Panthers to Tepper provided those on the NFL&amp;rsquo ; s finance committee are satisfied with the structure of the deal . Tepper is a minority owner of the Pittsburgh Steelers and already has been vetted and approved . He would be required to sell his stake in the Steelers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , the owners will begin making preparations for the widespread legalization of sports betting by states . The NFL previously had expressed opposition this , citing concerns about the integrity of the game . But with the Supreme Court&amp;rsquo ; s ruling , the league and owners have no choice but to accept the new landscape while beginning to devise a means to take advantage of potential new revenue streams . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Goodell said in a written statement released Monday that Congress must provide a framework by which widespread sports betting can exist without threatening the integrity of pro sports leagues . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have spent considerable time planning for the potential of broadly legalized sports gambling and are prepared to address these changes in a thoughtful and comprehensive way , including substantial education and compliance trainings for our clubs , players , employees and partners , &amp;rdquo ; Goodell said . &amp;ldquo ; These efforts include supporting common sense legislation that protects our players , coaches and fans and maintains public confidence in our games . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; Washington PostTue , 22 May 2018 14:45:21 **45;6454;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " Gregory Shamus " **28;7707;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77129290 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s Super Bowl set 17 statistical records and tied 12 more , according to research by the Elias Sports Bureau . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 1,151 combined total yards by the Eagles and Patriots obliterated the old record by 222 yards . Below is a histogram showing the combined total yards of the 52 Super Bowls , sorted into bins of 25 yards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;7737;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7772;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s game stands alone way out on the right . Next is a chart of the 12 Super Bowls with the highest combined total yards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;7802;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7837;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Research using the Play Index at **26;7867;TOOLONG shows Super Bowl LII is only the &lt;a href= " http : //pfref.com/tiny/Ztlsc " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;14th game&lt;/a&gt; &amp;nbsp ; since 1940 in which both teams recorded 500 or more total yards , and it was the 11th that for a game that didn&amp;rsquo ; t go to overtime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was only the second time it had been done in a post-season game . The first came Jan. 4 , 2014 , when Indianapolis beat Kansas City &lt;a href= " https : **33;7895;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;45-44&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , our **26;7930;TOOLONG research found that this game set an unofficial Super Bowl record for the most pass plays of 20 or more yards with 15 . The previous record was 12 by the game following the 1987 season , when Washington walked all over Denver 42-10 , and by the game following the 2012 season , when Baltimore defeated San Francisco 34-31 . Only seven Super Bowls in history have had 10 or more pass completions for 20 or more yards , although four have now come in the last six games . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Photo of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; A tale of two quarterbacks. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;7958;TOOLONG ... title= " New England Patriots quarterback Tom Brady reckons with his fourth-quarter fumble against the Philadelphia Eagles in Super Bowl LII on Feb. 4. " data-portal-copyright= " Elsa " **28;8058;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77128935 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;At **27;8088;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Ahead of the start of events in Pyeongchang , Joe O&amp;rsquo ; Connor spoke with a few of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " http : **34;8117;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;most prominent Olympic country hoppers&lt;/a&gt; &amp;mdash ; immigrant-athletes who left their birthland and found a home , a team and an opportunity here . &amp;ldquo ; Canada , to me , is like what Neil Young said in his song Harvest Moon , &amp;rdquo ; says Chris Spring , a transplanted bobsled pilot from Australia . &amp;ldquo ; &amp;lsquo ; When we were strangers , I liked you from afar . When we were lovers , I loved you with all my heart . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; From Cuba and East Germany to the fleetingly dominant Soviet Union , communist countries have traditionally excelled at the Olympics . But that &lt;a href= " http : **34;8153;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;is n't the case for North Korea&lt;/a&gt; , winners of a whopping two Winter Olympic medals &amp;mdash ; and none since 1992 . Pyongyang is often unable to send a single athlete to the Winter Games , writes Tristin Hopper , who set out to deduce why the North has never measured up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;TV tonight&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;All times Eastern&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 p.m. &lt;/strong&gt; Soccer : Premier League , Watford vs . Harvard **25;8274;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; ... p.m. &lt;/strong&gt; NBA : Utah at New Orleans &lt;em&gt;SN One&lt;/em&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;9 p.m. &lt;/strong&gt; NHL : Tampa Bay at Edmonton &lt;em&gt;SN West&lt;/em&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;10:3 ... p.m. &lt;/strong&gt; NBA : Dallas at LA Clippers &lt;em&gt;SN One&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Hot Buttered Post is served Monday through Thursday . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; National Post StaffMon , 05 Feb 2018 18:30:42 **45;8301;TOOLONG ... Eagles regain lead in dying minutes , beat New England Patriots to win first Super Bowlhttp : **36;8348;TOOLONG ... src= " https : **33;8386;TOOLONG ... title= " Philadelphia Eagles tight end Zach Ertz ( left ) scores a fourth-quarter touchdown against the New England Patriots in Super Bowl LII on Feb. 4. " alt= " Philadelphia Eagles tight end Zach Ertz ( left ) scores a fourth-quarter touchdown against the New England Patriots in Super Bowl LII on Feb. 4. " data-portal-copyright= " Gregory Shamus " **28;8421;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77128139 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; MINNEAPOLIS &amp;mdash ; Brady , Shmady. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The greatest , most accomplished quarterback in NFL history put forth another Super Bowl performance for the ages , but this time it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t enough . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the largest explosion of offence in Super Bowl history , the Philadelphia Eagles on Sunday night outfought Brady&amp;rsquo ; s New England Patriots , deservedly capturing the Philly franchise&amp;rsquo ; s first Super Bowl championship , 41-33. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both teams combined for an NFL championship-game record of 1,151 total yards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This long-elusive Philadelphia victory thrilled most of the decidedly pro-Eagles crowd at the Midwest&amp;rsquo ; s monument to girders and glass , 19-month-old U.S. Bank Stadium . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Eagles hadn&amp;rsquo ; t won an NFL championship since before the Super Bowl era began &amp;mdash ; in 1960 , with a defeat of legendary Vince Lombardi&amp;rsquo ; s Green Bay Packers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now , they&amp;rsquo ; ve got their first Vince Lombardi Trophy &amp;mdash ; given annually to the Super Bowl champion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Quarterback Nick ( Nick Who ? ) Foles was named MVP of Super Bowl LII . He headed the list of offensive heroes for the Eagles&amp;rsquo ; unstoppable offence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have such a great group of guys , &amp;rdquo ; Foles told NBC on the euphoric post-game champion&amp;rsquo ; s podium , only about 10 yards from where his tight end , Zach Ertz , caught his game-winning touchdown throw with 2:21 left . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That was Foles&amp;rsquo ; third touchdown pass of the game . Inexplicably , in just his fourth career playoff game , and only his sixth start since the Eagles&amp;rsquo ; superstar second-year QB Carson Wentz was lost for the season on Dec. 10 with a blown-out knee , Foles played superbly , matching Brady clutch throw for clutch throw throughout , if not yard for yard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brady threw for three touchdowns without an interception and 505 yards . The latter mark broke the single-game Super Bowl record he set just a year ago , 466. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foles , a sixth-year pro in his second stint with the Eagles , threw a few unwise passes in this gigantic indoor stadium , and got away with perhaps more than his share . But on third downs and , especially , on New England&amp;rsquo ; s side of the field he performed spectacularly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; To be part of this organization , to be in this moment is something you dream about as a kid , &amp;rdquo ; Foles said . &amp;ldquo ; To be right here with the confetti flying with the greatest group of men &amp;hellip ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for long-suffering Philadelphians ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have been wanting this for a long time , &amp;rdquo ; Foles said , &amp;ldquo ; and I am just blessed to be a part of it . We have such a great group of guys . Such a great city to play for and I am proud to be a Philadelphia Eagle . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foles finished 28-of-43 for 373 yards , three touchdowns and only one interception . He also caught a touchdown pass on probably the greatest goal-line call in Super Bowl history , a play sure to go down in NFL lore . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With 34 seconds left before halftime , on 4th-and goal from the one , Eagles head coach Doug Pederson went for it . He had Foles line up in the pistol ( the short shotgun ) with third-string running back Corey Clement behind him . Then Foles repositioned himself as a wingback to the right . Clement took the direct snap , ran to his left , and handed off to backup tight end Trey Burton on an end-around back to the right . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Burton then slowed down and threw a pass to a wide-open Foles , who&amp;rsquo ; d filtered out into the right flat of the end zone , uncovered . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The quarterback caught the pass for a startlingly cool touchdown. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I hadn&amp;rsquo ; t thrown a touchdown pass since high school , &amp;rdquo ; Burton said in the incredibly jubilant Eagles locker room . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Eagles never trailed until Brady hit his trusted tight end , Rob Gronkowski , with a four-yard corner lob with 9:22 left . That gave New England a 33-32 lead , after earlier trailing as much as 15-3 and 22-12 in the second quarter , and 29-19 in the third . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foles showed steel-spine courage , though , in rallying the Eagles &amp;ndash ; on what surely will be remembered as the greatest drive in franchise history &amp;ndash ; to what proved to be the winning touchdown with 2:21 left . Foles hit tight end Zach Ertz on an 11-yard scoring slant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Up 38-33 , the Eagles went for two and didn&amp;rsquo ; t get it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It didn&amp;rsquo ; t matter . One play after the kickoff , following an eight-yard completion to Gronkowski , Brady was stripped of the ball by fellow University of Michigan alum Brandon Graham , Philadelphia&amp;rsquo ; s leading sacker in the regular season . Rookie defensive end Derek Barnett recovered the ball at the New England 33 , with 2:09 left . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After three running plays the Eagles went up 41-33 on Jake Elliott&amp;rsquo ; s 46-yard field goal , with 1:05 left . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brady passed the Patriots from his own nine only as far as midfield in the dying seconds . After his Hail Mary heave on the game&amp;rsquo ; s last play fell incomplete in the Philly end zone , it was over . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canons sounded , and the green-and-white confetti machines went to work . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before the end of the third quarter the offences combined for the most total yards in the 52-year history of the Super Bowl . Philly finished with 538 , New England with 613. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both defences performed awfully in the first half , mailing it in almost as much as Justin Timberlake did at halftime . Alas , defences can not pre-record their performances , as it sounded like JT did . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Defenders got gashed again in the second half . They left receivers wide open all over the field , flailed meekly at tackle attempts ( ProFootballFocus.com counted 10 by the Patriots alone ) and made just about every offensive performer look like an all-pro. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But credit each team&amp;rsquo ; s offensive play-callers : Pats offensive co-ordinator Josh McDaniels and Eagles head coach and chief offensive strategist , Pederson . Both called masterful games . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although Brady threw for a startling 267 yards in the first half alone , Foles nearly matched him &amp;ndash ; completing 13-of-22 for 215 yards and a touchdown. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The first-half offensive fireworks immediately continued following the elongated halftime break . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each team scored a touchdown on its opening possession , as Brady and Foles kept throwing darts into open receivers&amp;rsquo ; hands . The difference for Philadelphia is that running backs LeGarrette Blount and Jay Ajayi kept bludgeoning the interior New England defence for huge gains . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Postmedia NetworkMon , 05 Feb 2018 05:26:15 **45;8451;TOOLONG ... celebrates Super Bowl week with three Americans who know nothing about footballhttp : **32;8498;TOOLONG ... src= " https : **33;8532;TOOLONG ... title= " Alex Trebek. Telecast info is available in the TV listings below . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Kevin Martin will be inducted into the World Curling Hall of Fame later this season , the World Curling Federation announced today . The Edmonton curler skipped Canada to two Olympic medals &amp;mdash ; silver at Salt Lake City 2002 and gold at Vancouver 2010 &amp;mdash ; and retired from the sport in 2014 with a record 18 victories in Grand Slam events . Martin won the Brier in 1991 , 1997 , 2008 and 2009 ; he added a gold medal at the world championships in 2008 and finished second there in 1991 and 2009. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The Canadian Baseball Hall of Fame will enshrine Pedro Martinez and Lloyd Moseby in June , it announced today . Martinez , a first-ballot inductee to Cooperstown in 2015 , pitched for the Expos from 1994-97 and won his first of three Cy Young Awards in his final season with Montreal . Moseby played the first 10 of his 12 MLB seasons with the Blue Jays , manning centre field in between George Bell and Jesse Barfield for much of the 1980s . The class of 2018 also includes Canadian baseball historian William Humber . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;8918;TOOLONG ... title= " In this June 26 , 2013 file photo , former Toronto Blue Jays outfielder Lloyd Moseby keeps his eye on the ball at the Roberto Alomar &amp; Friends Charity Home Run Derby in Vancouver . Vegas would stand alone as the team with the most wins by an expansion team with a victory tonight in Winnipeg . They tied the record of 33 this week in just their 49th game . No NHL expansion team has ever finished the season with a winning record ; the Knights are currently 33-12-4 for 70 points . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; &amp;nbsp ; The NHL reports , from research by Elias , that Maple Leafs rookies Travis Dermott and Justin Holl are the fourth set of defencemen since 1991-92 to record their first career NHL goal in the same game . They were preceded by Detroit&amp;rsquo ; s Nicklas Lidstrom and Vladimir Konstantinov in 1991 , by Edmonton&amp;rsquo ; s Adam Bennett and Ilya Byakin in 1993 and by Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Shane Hnidy and Karel Rachunek in 2001. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Russian World Cup officials swear the tournament won&amp;rsquo ; t be overrun by marauding locusts later this summer . Chief organizer Alexei Sorokin went on record today to dispute a claim from Russia&amp;rsquo ; s agricultural ministry that the insects could descend on stadiums in the southern part of the country this summer and gorge on the grass . Sorokin said locusts won&amp;rsquo ; t be a problem because the World Cup fields will be cut short and &amp;ldquo ; sprayed with certain substances. &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; We try to look at this with humour , &amp;rdquo ; he added . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not a threat . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Toronto FC brought a player with World Cup experience into the fold today , signing Dutch international Gregory van der Wiel from Cagliari of Italy&amp;rsquo ; s Serie A. The 29-year-old defender will likely become Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s starting right back following Steven Beitashour&amp;rsquo ; s departure to Los Angeles FC . Van der Wiel has 46 caps with the Netherlands , including appearances at the 2010 World Cup , where they lost in the final to Spain , and at Euro 2012 . He&amp;rsquo ; s spent the bulk of his pro career at Ajax ( 2007-12 ) and Paris St. Germain ( 2012-16 ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;8983;TOOLONG ... title= " In this Feb. 13 , 2016 file photo , PSG defender Gregory van der Wiel controls the ball against Lille . " alt= " In this Feb. 13 , 2016 file photo , PSG defender Gregory van der Wiel controls the ball against Lille . " data-portal-copyright= " Thibault Camus " **28;9018;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77122265 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; &amp;nbsp ; Peter Polansky has been selected to play singles for Canada at the Davis Cup tie in Osijek , Croatia , this weekend , supplanting Vasek Pospisil , who has been ill with a virus recently . Pospisil still will play doubles with Daniel Nestor on Saturday . Polansky , ranked No. 141 in the world , meets world No. 47 Borna Coric on the indoor clay court on Friday at 8 a.m . ET , followed by Denis Shapovalov ( No. 48 ) against Viktor Galovic ( No. 181 ) . The singles matchups will be reversed on Sunday , with Shapovalov up first . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; &amp;nbsp ; Tennis Canada announced yesterday that Australian Open mixed doubles champion Gabriela Dabrowski will headline the Fed Cup team playing Romania on the road Feb. 10-11 . She will be joined by Carol Zhao , Bianca Andreescu and Katherine Sebov. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; &amp;nbsp ; Bodog reports that its most popular Super Bowl prop bet so far is the MVP race , with Patriots quarterback Tom Brady taking 22 per cent of the action . People are also apparently betting the over-under on how many times Brady&amp;rsquo ; s wife , Gisele Bundchen , will be shown on TV during the game . The line is at 1.5 times , with 81 per cent betting the over so far . Seventy per cent of the money bet on whether Justin Timberlake will wear a hat during his halftime show is going to the yes side . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9048;TOOLONG ... title= " New England Patriots quarterback Tom Brady celebrates his team 's win in the AFC Championship Game on Jan. 21. " alt= " New England Patriots quarterback Tom Brady celebrates his team 's win in the AFC Championship Game on Jan. 21. " data-portal-copyright= " Maddie Meyer " **28;9083;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77122019 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Jan. 23 , Sports Illustrated posted the following tweet , which got us thinking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;blockquote class= " twitter-tweet " data-width= " 550 " data-dnt= " true " &gt;&lt;p lang= " en " dir= " ltr " &gt;Fourteen years ago , a 7-year-old in Foxboro told Tom Brady he had named his baby brother after him . ' He was pretty young then . It 's probably something he hears once or twice a week now . ' &lt;a href= " https : //t.co/VDHRoyxxsQ " &gt;https : //t.c ... -- Sports Illustrated ( @SInow ) &lt;a href= " https : **33;9113;TOOLONG ... 23 , **33;9148;TOOLONG &lt;script async src= " https : **33;9183;TOOLONG " charset= " utf-8 " &gt;&lt;/script&gt; &lt;p&gt; Just how many babies get named after Super Bowl-winning quarterbacks ? If there are three things America loves , it&amp;rsquo ; s the Super Bowl , quarterbacks and babies . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The U.S. government offers &lt;a href= " https : **33;9218;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;baby name data&lt;/a&gt; &amp;nbsp ; taken from Social Security applications , broken down by state and going back to 1901. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Massachusetts , the name &amp;ldquo ; Brady&amp;rdquo ; appears in the data only four times prior to 1990 , accounting for 35 applications . It appears every year from 1990 on , but was used only 153 times in the 1990s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below is a chart showing the usage of the names Thomas and Brady in Massachusetts since Tom Brady was drafted by the Patriots in the sixth round in 2000 . For the purposes of these charts , we are labelling the years teams appeared in the Super Bowl . The Patriots appeared in XXXVI on Feb. 3 , 2002 following the 2001 NFL season , so 2002 is the labelled year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9253;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9288;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Brady&amp;rdquo ; remains low in 2000 and 2001 , but it shoots up to 90 in 2002 after Brady replaced Drew Bledsoe and won his first Super Bowl . It moved up slightly in 2003 , then rose 65 per cent after the second victory at the start of 2004 . Another win in 2005 brought it closer to 200 and 49th overall among boys names in the state . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 2007 , the season the Patriots put together their undefeated regular season , the name Brady broke through the 200 level and was ranked 34th . This was the Brady heyday . It has been in decline since then , falling to 50th in 2008 . By 2010 , Brady was no longer in triple digits , and it has remained relatively static through 2016 regardless of the Patriots&amp;rsquo ; fortunes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The name Thomas , however , has rebounded over the last three years available after a long , slow decline . It was the 17th-ranked name for boys in 2016. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Are we able to find similar results for other quarterback names in other states ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Looking at Bens and Benjamins in Pennsylvania has no value because the name is popular on its own . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below is a chart for Russells in Washington state . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9318;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9353;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The name Russell reached its peak in Washington after the Second World War , but dwindled back into the 10s and 20s by the mid-1990s . But the name has enjoyed a modest renaissance , reaching 50 in 2014 , the year Russell Wilson led the Seahawks to the win in Super Bowl XLVII against Denver . Another 44 boys received the name in 2015 , showing prospective parents blamed Pete Carroll rather than Wilson for the interception at the goal line to end the game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The name Peyton offers an interesting case in two states . Here&amp;rsquo ; s a chart&amp;nbsp ; reflecting Peyton Manning&amp;rsquo ; s long tenure in Indiana . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9383;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9418;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The name Peyton doesn&amp;rsquo ; t even show up in the data until 1992 and it isn&amp;rsquo ; t until 1998 , the year Manning was drafted , that it becomes more common as a boy&amp;rsquo ; s name . The usage for boys peaks in 2006 , the year Manning led the Colts to their first Super Bowl victory since moving from Baltimore in 1984 . Female Peytons outnumber males again starting in 2011 , the year Manning sat out recovering from neck surgery before leaving the organization . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now let&amp;rsquo ; s look at whether Peyton became a thing in Colorado . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9448;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9483;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It did not , despite Super Bowl appearances in 2014 and 2016. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now comes the quarterback with perhaps the most interesting effect on baby naming in two states . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9513;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9548;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We limited the scope here just to the name Eli rather than extend it to other forms of the name . Elijah would be a more common long form , but Eli Manning&amp;rsquo ; s given name is Elisha. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Eli receives a bit of a bump in New York state following Manning&amp;rsquo ; s draft in 2004 . It spikes in New York in 2006 , the year of Manning&amp;rsquo ; s first Super Bowl win . It continues to rise , though , which may indicate a general interest in the name apart from Manning . Or it could reflect the popularity of the Giants ; it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to tell . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We do see another rise following the 2012 Super Bowl win and continued strength of the name . But is it all Eli Manning ? Or is it because many babies look like Eli Manning , sort of nondescript and mushy with strange expressions we can&amp;rsquo ; t read ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9578;TOOLONG ... title= " New York Giants quarterback Eli Manning stands on the sidelines against the Arizona Cardinals on Dec. 24 , 2017. " alt= " New York Giants quarterback Eli Manning stands on the sidelines against the Arizona Cardinals on Dec. 24 , 2017. " data-portal-copyright= " Rick Scuteri " **28;9613;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77122028 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Photo of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; In which a Manchester United player strikes the universal pose for &amp;ldquo ; scored on own net . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;9643;TOOLONG ... title= " Manchester United defender Phil Jones bemoans his own goal against Tottenham at Wembley Stadium in London on Jan. 31. " data-portal-copyright= " Patrick Smith " **28;10907;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77119667 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;10937;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;10972;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Your midday sports snack . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Toast points&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Here&amp;rsquo ; s a funny , Raptors-related nugget from James Harden&amp;rsquo ; s 60-point triple-double against the Magic last night : he contributed to as many of his team&amp;rsquo ; s points &amp;mdash ; 86 &amp;mdash ; as Kobe Bryant did when he scored 81 by himself in January 2006 . The difference , of course , is that Harden&amp;rsquo ; s 11 assists led to 26 Rockets points , while Bryant only dished out two assists for five points that fateful night against Toronto . The NBA record of 104 came on another two-assist night , notes the Elias Sports Bureau : Wilt Chamberlain&amp;rsquo ; s 100-point game against the Knicks in March 1962. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Per Elias , Harden&amp;rsquo ; s career-high 60 points ( to go along with the 11 assists and 10 rebounds ) were the most in a triple-double performance in NBA history , surpassing Russell Westbrook&amp;rsquo ; s 57 points against the Magic last March . Entering last night , Harden was already leading the league in scoring this season at 30.9 points per game . His average is now 31.6. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;11002;TOOLONG ... title= " Houston Rockets guard James Harden ( right ) high-fives forward Ryan Anderson against the Orlando Magic on Jan. 30. " alt= " Houston Rockets guard James Harden ( right ) high-fives forward Ryan Anderson against the Orlando Magic on Jan. 30. " data-portal-copyright= " Eric Christian Smith " **28;11037;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77119642 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; It has been a busy 16 or so hours in hockey . Avalanche centre Nathan MacKinnon , the NHL&amp;rsquo ; s second-leading scorer , suffered an apparent shoulder injury against the Canucks last night and will miss at least one game . The Golden Knights tied an expansion record with their 33nd win of the season &amp;mdash ; with 33 games remaining . Jaromir Jagr officially signed with his hometown club , Kladno , in the Czech Republic today . And Mike Fisher will end his short-lived retirement sometime before the Feb. 26 trade deadline and rejoin the Predators for one last shot at the Stanley Cup , the team said this afternoon . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Four berths in the championship round are on the line in both pools today at the Scotties Tournament of Hearts . All eight teams in Pool A have two games left to play : Jennifer Jones ( Manitoba ) and Kerri Einarson ( the wild card ) are ahead of the pack at 5-0 and 4-1 , respectively , while Nova Scotia and Northern Ontario are 3-2 and Saskatchewan and New Brunswick are 2-3 . Everyone in Pool B has one game left : Casey Scheidegger ( Alberta ) and Michelle Englot ( Canada ) are 5-1 , Stacie Curtis ( Newfoundland and Labrador ) is 4-2 , and B.C. , Ontario and Quebec are all 3-3. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;11067;TOOLONG ... title= " Manitoba skip Jennifer Jones curls against New Brunswick at the Scotties Tournament of Hearts on Jan. 30. " alt= " Manitoba skip Jennifer Jones curls against New Brunswick at the Scotties Tournament of Hearts on Jan. 30. " data-portal-copyright= " Sean Kilpatrick " **28;11102;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77119644 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Police in Philadelphia have a secret substance they believe will keep fans from climbing the city&amp;rsquo ; s infrastructure following Super Bowl LII on Sunday . Police commissioner Richard Ross won&amp;rsquo ; t divulge the substance , which the force will use instead of the vegetable shortening it tried , somewhat ineffectively , for the NFC conference championship . The goal of police , AP reports , is to assure the safety of fans &amp;ldquo ; as well as the people who they could fall down on , &amp;rdquo ; Ross said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Indigenous activist Douglas Cardinal plans to proceed with his human rights complaints against the Cleveland Indians despite the team&amp;rsquo ; s decision to remove the red-faced Chief Wahoo logo from its caps and jerseys in 2019 . Cardinal alleged racial discrimination in complaints he filed to the Canadian Human Rights Tribunal and the Ontario Human Rights Commission in October 2016 , as Cleveland faced the Blue Jays in the ALCS. &amp;ldquo ; We just want to get rid of the logo , the name and everything , the whole reference , &amp;rdquo ; he told The Canadian Press yesterday . The CHRT has yet to decide if it will hear Cardinal&amp;rsquo ; s case , while his lawyer said the OHRC has set hearings for July . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The fate of dozens of Russian athletes banned from the Pyeongchang Olympics will be revealed overnight around 3 a.m . ET , when the Court of Arbitration for Sport announces its rulings on 39 appeals . CAS appellate panels held hearings in Geneva last week , after an IOC panel decided to retroactively disqualify the 39 athletes for doping offences at Sochi 2014 . Some of them could compete in Pyeongchang if their bans are overturned , even though the IOC deadline for team lists to be finalized was last weekend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;11132;TOOLONG ... title= " Philadelphia Eagles quarterback Nick Foles celebrates a touchdown against the Minnesota Vikings in the NFC Championship Game on Jan. 21. " alt= " Philadelphia Eagles quarterback Nick Foles celebrates a touchdown against the Minnesota Vikings in the NFC Championship Game on Jan. 21. " data-portal-copyright= " Patrick Smith " **28;11167;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77119667 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Eagles&amp;rsquo ; Nick Foles is attempting to become the eighth backup quarterback to play a significant playoff role on a team that wins the Super Bowl . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foles took over as the starter in Week 15 after Carson Wentz tore his ACL against the Rams . Since then has thrown for 1,037 yards in five starts , including playoff wins over Atlanta and Minnesota . Philadelphia , which might have expected its hold on the top seed in the NFC could be in jeopardy without Wentz , has not missed a beat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foles&amp;rsquo ; exploits bring to mind names from the past like Jeff Hostetler , the Giants quarterback who saved the season in 1990 by going on a five-game run in place of an injured Phil Simms , culminating in a 20-19 win over Buffalo in Super Bowl XXV. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below are two charts showing the playoff passing yards and the playoff quarterback rating for each player in his Super Bowl run . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;11197;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;11232;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;11262;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;11297;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Not everyone of the players on the list below were late-season heroes . Let&amp;rsquo ; s examine the players and their circumstances . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Earl Morrall&lt;/strong&gt; : The career backup from Muskegon , Mich. , played 21 seasons in the NFL yet only had four years in which he started more than 10 games . He was , perhaps , the greatest backup in the history of the league , especially late in his career when he saved seasons for both the Baltimore Colts and the Miami Dolphins . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 1968 , Morrall had already been in the league 13 years when he joined the Colts and was thrust into the leading role because of a pre-season injury to Johnny Unitas . Morrell&amp;rsquo ; s Colts compiled a 13-1 record and won the NFL championship game , but suffered the first great upset in Super Bowl history at the hands of Joe Namath and the New York Jets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Morrall replaced Unitas in Super Bowl V after the starter bruised his ribs in the second quarter against Dallas . Baltimore , now in the AFC after the leagues merged , trailed 13-6 when Morrall entered the game for the last series before halftime . The Colts benefited from two interceptions of Craig Morton deep in Cowboys territory that led to 10 points , including Jim O&amp;rsquo ; Brien&amp;rsquo ; s 32-yard field goal with nine seconds left in the game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When head coach Don Shula left Baltimore for Miami , he didn&amp;rsquo ; t hesitate to add Morrall to the roster for the 1972 season . Starter Bob Griese was injured in Game 5 and Morrall led the Dolphins the rest of the way to their perfect 14-0 regular season . Morrall started the first two playoff games but gave way to Griese in the Super Bowl , which Miami won 14-7 over Washington to cap the league&amp;rsquo ; s only completely perfect season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jim Plunkett&lt;/strong&gt; : He resurrected his career in 1980 after eight mostly mediocre seasons in New England and San Francisco . He joined the Raiders in 1979 and was asked to take over from an ineffective Dan Pastorini in Week 5 . Oakland won nine of their last 11 regular-season games and Plunkett helped them become the first wild-card team to win the Super Bowl . He threw three touchdown passes , including one of 80 yards to Kenny King to defeat Philadelphia 27-10. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Doug Williams&lt;/strong&gt; : After a two-year stint in the USFL , Williams returned to the NFL with Washington to back up Jay Schroeder . Williams only started two games in 1987 , both losses , but came off the bench in three others , including the season finale , all wins . Head coach Joe Gibbs went with Williams as the playoff starter and he reeled off three straight wins , including a 42-10 crushing of Denver in Super Bowl XXII . Williams , the first black quarterback to start an NFL title game , threw four second-quarter touchdown passes to bury the Broncos by halftime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;span class= " embed-youtube " style= " text-align:center ; display : block ; " &gt;&lt;iframe class='youtube-player ' type='text/html ' width= ' 640 ' height= ' 390 ' src='https : **34;11327;TOOLONG ... allowfullscreen='true ' style='border:0 ; ' **28;11363;TOOLONG ... &lt;strong&gt;Jeff Hostetler&lt;/strong&gt; : He had been a backup in New York for five seasons and was contemplating retirement late in the 1990 season when he came in to replace Simms , who had a foot injury , in the second-last game of the season . The Giants won their final two regular-season games to finish 13-3 . Hostetler was injured himself in the NFC championship game against the 49ers , but he came back later to lead two scoring drives in the 15-13 win . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Super Bowl XXV against Buffalo , which was making the first of its four straight appearances in the big game , Hostetler threw a second-quarter touchdown pass and had four completions on the fourth-quarter drive that led to Matt Bahr&amp;rsquo ; s field goal . He then turned the one-point lead over to the defence and the bedeviled Scott Norwood&amp;rsquo ; s right foot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;span class= " embed-youtube " style= " text-align:center ; display : block ; " &gt;&lt;iframe class='youtube-player ' type='text/html ' width= ' 640 ' height= ' 390 ' src='https : **34;11393;TOOLONG ... allowfullscreen='true ' style='border:0 ; ' **28;11429;TOOLONG ... &lt;strong&gt;Kurt Warner&lt;/strong&gt; : His path through the Arena Football League and NFL Europe is well told , leading Warner to be Trent Green&amp;rsquo ; s untested backup in 1999 . Green tore an ACL in a pre-season game , Warner was thrust into the spotlight and a Hall of Fame career was born . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Rams offence under Warner became known as the Greatest Show on Turf . Warner was the league MVP , leading the Rams to a 13-3 record . He is far and away the leader among our championship backups with 1,063 passing yards and the only one with a playoff passer rating of 100 . And yet the Rams still could have gone home empty-handed from Super Bowl XXXIV had linebacker Mike Jones not tackled Titans receiver Kevin Dyson on the one-yard line to end the game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;span class= " embed-youtube " style= " text-align:center ; display : block ; " &gt;&lt;iframe class='youtube-player ' type='text/html ' width= ' 640 ' height= ' 390 ' src='https : **34;11459;TOOLONG ... allowfullscreen='true ' style='border:0 ; ' **28;11495;TOOLONG ... &lt;strong&gt;Trent Dilfer&lt;/strong&gt; : The Ravens quarterback remains a punchline among some fans who decry his anemic performances on the way to Baltimore&amp;rsquo ; s win in Super Bowl XXXV . Differ took over as the starter from Tony Banks for the ninth game of the season ; after a 9-6 loss to Pittsburgh , he rolled to seven straight wins . The Ravens had to win four playoff game as a wild card and Dilfer didn&amp;rsquo ; t throw for more than 200 yards in any of them . But he didn&amp;rsquo ; t need to . He only threw one playoff interception and the defence did the rest , allowing only 23 points in those four games . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Baltimore scored four different ways in the Super Bowl against the Giants , including an interception return and a kickoff return . But Dilfer opened the scoring with a 38-yard scoring pass to Brandon Stokley and has a ring to soften the snide remarks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tom Brady&lt;/strong&gt; : It has been forgotten amid the legend of Tom Brady that he was not the Patriots&amp;rsquo ; starting quarterback to start the 2001 season . Drew Bledsoe was the incumbent , but Brady was given the job in the third game and hasn&amp;rsquo ; t been usurped yet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brady only threw for 145 yards in Super Bowl XXXVI to Warner&amp;rsquo ; s 365 . But like he&amp;rsquo ; s done countless times since , Brady kept the Patriots close until the end . He had five completions on the final drive of the game after the Rams tied the score at 17-17 with 1:37 left . Brady got the Patriots to the Rams&amp;rsquo ; 30 , setting up Adam Vinatieri&amp;rsquo ; s winning 48-yard field goal with seven seconds left . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;11525;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " autoplay ; encrypted-media " **34;11561;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt; ... of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Olympics are only nine days away . Jagr is expected to rejoin his hometown team , HC Kladno of the Czech second division , for whom he played during his junior days and over parts of two NHL lockouts . The 45-year-old winger departs having played 1,733 regular-season games for nine teams , six of them in the last seven seasons . His 1,921 career points are second only to Wayne Gretzky , while his 766 goals only trail Gretzky and Gordie Howe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;12239;TOOLONG ... title= " In this Nov. 9 , 2017 file photo , Jaromir Jagr celebrates his first ? ? ? and only ? ? ? goal with the Calgary Flames . Boston&amp;rsquo ; s Brad Stevens participated last season , so he&amp;rsquo ; s out . Stephen Curry picked DeMar DeRozan and Kyle Lowry for his team through the NBA&amp;rsquo ; s new all-star draft last week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The last two sport federations , Alpine Canada and Cross-Country Canada , nominated their athletes for the Olympic team today . With the addition of 14 alpine skiers and 11 cross-country skiers , Canada&amp;rsquo ; s list for Pyeongchang , unofficially by our count , will contain 220 names . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Four teams are still undefeated through the opening weekend of play at the Scotties Tournament of Hearts in Penticton , B.C. " data-portal-copyright= " Steven Senne " **28;12339;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77114710 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Predictably , Tom Brady wasn&amp;rsquo ; t amused when a Boston radio host called his five-year-old daughter an &amp;ldquo ; annoying little pissant. &amp;rdquo ; Alex Reimer made the remark on WEEI after watching the first episode of Tom vs . He capped the morning with an actual mic drop , cajoling fans into a frenzy at the Patriots&amp;rsquo ; sendoff rally in Foxborough. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;blockquote class= " twitter-tweet " data-width= " 550 " data-dnt= " true " &gt;&lt;p lang= " en " dir= " ltr " &gt;Tom Brady literally dropped the mic at the Patriots ' send-off rally . &lt;a href= " https : //t.co/616vOBVF1X " &gt;pic.twitter ... -- SportsCenter ( @SportsCenter ) &lt;a href= " https : **33;12369;TOOLONG ... 29 , **33;12404;TOOLONG &lt;script async src= " https : **33;12439;TOOLONG " charset= " utf-8 " &gt;&lt;/script&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Now that Super Bowl Week is here , the NFL title game is only six days away . Sports are weird sometimes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Bell Media officially announced the second edition of its $300,000 effort to get viewers to watch Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s Super Bowl on its networks . After the CRTC decided Canadians had a cultural right to watch American Super Bowl commercials prior to the 2017 game , Bell offered a contest that required people to watch its broadcast of the game to win cash and prizes . Advertisements for this year&amp;rsquo ; s contest ran during programming over the weekend and Bell issued an official press release today . TSN&amp;rsquo ; s Natasha Staniszewski and Tessa Bonhomme will host . Bell will maximize your ability to see the program by airing the game itself on CTV , CTV Two and TSN2. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;12474;TOOLONG ... title= " Bo Bichette of the U.S. Team bats against the World Team during the SiriusXM All-Star Futures Game at Marlins Park on July 9 , 2017. " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;12509;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77092684 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We took a snapshot of **25;12539;TOOLONG ; s top 100 prospects earlier this month , which reflected the ranking following the Angels&amp;rsquo ; signing of Shohei Ohtani in early December . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Saturday night , baseball&amp;rsquo ; s in-house prospect service released its latest ranking heading into spring training in three weeks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here&amp;rsquo ; s the original chart : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;12566;TOOLONG ... class= " aligncenter size-full wp-image-77076235 " src= " https : **33;12601;TOOLONG ... alt= " " width= " 640 " height= " 640 " srcset= " https : **31;12636;TOOLONG ... 640w , https : **39;12669;TOOLONG ... 150w , https : **39;12710;TOOLONG ... 300w " sizes= " ( max-width : 640px ) 100vw , 640px " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And here&amp;rsquo ; s the updated version : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;12751;TOOLONG ... class= " aligncenter size-full wp-image-77114130 " src= " https : **33;12786;TOOLONG ... alt= " " width= " 640 " height= " 640 " srcset= " https : **31;12821;TOOLONG ... 640w , https : **39;12854;TOOLONG ... 150w , https : **39;12895;TOOLONG ... 300w " sizes= " ( max-width : 640px ) 100vw , 640px " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the Blue Jays , Vladimir Guerrero Jr. , moved up one spot to No. 3 and Anthony Alford dropped three spots to No. 47 . As expected , Bo Bichette climbed significantly , from No. 26 to No. 14 . That makes the Blue Jays one of only four teams with two prospects in the top 20 and one of only nine with at least three players in the top 50. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Braves broke the previous four-way tie at the top and now have eight prospects in the top 100 . The Padres remain with seven and are joined by the White Sox and Yankees . The Rays lost a player from the top 100 and now have six , along with the Phillies. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Brewers took the biggest hit , dropping from seven prospects to three . Milwaukee sent four prospects to Miami last week to acquire outfielder Christian Yelich , although only two appeared in the top 100 , including their top guy , Lewis Brinson . The deal allowed the Marlins to make an appearance on the new list after previously being one of four teams without a player in the top 100 . Two of the other players in the deal switched places in the ranking , with Isan Diaz dropping out and Monte Harrison moving in. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The only other teams that added or subtracted more than one prospect in the total were the Dodgers and Twins , both down two , and the Angels , who gained two . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among the players at the top of the list , the first six remained the same , although the order changed , with Atlanta&amp;rsquo ; s Ronald Acuna moving up four spots to No. 2 and the Yankees&amp;rsquo ; Gleyber Torres and Washington&amp;rsquo ; s Victor Robles both dropping three spots . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; San Diego&amp;rsquo ; s Fernando Tatis Jr. , made one of the biggest jumps , moving 44 spots to No. 8 . The Orioles&amp;rsquo ; Austin Hays had the biggest gain , going from No. 89 to No. 23. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Photos of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; One consistently entertaining feature of tennis majors is the scenic post-tournament photoshoot with the men&amp;rsquo ; s and women&amp;rsquo ; s singles champions . After the French Open , the winners have posed with their trophy in front of the Eiffel Tower . After the U.S. Open , they have basked in the September sunshine at New York City&amp;rsquo ; s Central Park . " alt= " WWE chairman and CEO Vince McMahon really wants to be the guy who launches a rival football league . " data-portal-copyright= " Jessica Hill " **28;13717;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77107872 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vince McMahon is bringing back the XFL . It&amp;rsquo ; s just like the old saying goes : If at first you don&amp;rsquo ; t succeed , wait 20 years and then try again , but this time do a lot of things the exact opposite of the way you tried them the first time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That may not be exactly how the saying goes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There was a point a couple of months ago , when rumours first started that McMahon , the wildly rich wrestling impresario , was going to restart his failed football venture , that you could at least squint at it a certain way and get a sense of what he was trying to do . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The National Football League has had some TV-ratings problems of late , and it has had the long-running controversy over certain players kneeling during the national anthem , and some people think the sport is ruining itself by trying to eliminate dangerous hits . As it happens , the President of the United States has criticized the NFL for all of those things . McMahon&amp;rsquo ; s wife , Linda , is in the Trump cabinet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In that light , you could see where McMahon might have been headed . The original XFL , which launched in 2001 and also died in 2001 , was infamously pro-violence . There were no fair catches on punts , and the coin toss was replaced with an opening tussle for the ball ( seriously ! ) , and there was a general vibe that this was FOOTBALL , dammit . The links to McMahon&amp;rsquo ; s wrestling business were overt , and you got the sense that if he could figure out a way to allow the football players to brain each other with a steel chair , he would have done it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So it was reasonable to assume that the new XFL would have tried to recapture some of that : A wilder league for football fans who , like Donald Trump , think the NFL has gone soft . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Instead , McMahon announced on Thursday that the XFL , which would begin play in 2020 with eight teams and a 10-game schedule , would be football &amp;ldquo ; re-imagined. &amp;rdquo ; The imagination part , oddly , is mostly still to come . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On a conference call with media , McMahon said the main goal of the XFL is &amp;ldquo ; to give football back to fans . &amp;rdquo ; If you can figure out what that means , you are ahead of me . The WWE boss said it would be a safer version of football , where medical expertise would be sought , but he also said it would be faster-paced , and the sizzle-reel video that launched on Thursday also promised &amp;ldquo ; fewer infractions. &amp;rdquo ; McMahon also said that one of the ideas under consideration is no halftime period , in an effort to speed up the game to a tidy two hours . So : fewer penalties , and no rest and recovery at halftime , but also &amp;hellip ; safer ? These things seem hard to square . Are the games going to be played in a bouncy castle ? A giant ball pit ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;13747;TOOLONG ... title= " Eighteen years ago , Vince McMahon was announcing the first XFL , which expired shortly after its inaugural season in 2001. " alt= " Eighteen years ago , Vince McMahon was announcing the first XFL , which expired shortly after its inaugural season in 2001. " data-portal-copyright= " Ezio Petersen " **28;13782;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77107875 " /&gt; &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13812;TOOLONG ... failed miserably after just one season in 2001 , but WWE 's Vince McMahon is bringing back the **35;13848;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Where the original XFL was a joint venture with the broadcaster NBC , which bailed after one season because the ratings had tanked , McMahon says he is going alone on this . He&amp;rsquo ; s investing US$100 million of his own money in the new XFL , and his new company Alpha Entertainment will own all the franchises and set all the rules , likely including one that will require players to stand during the national anthem . Asked on the conference call what kinds of opportunities there would be for crossovers between the WWE and XFL , McMahon said none whatsoever . This time , he&amp;rsquo ; s keeping the football business totally separate , an indication that if the XFL ends up being the equivalent of McMahon setting $100 million on fire , the WWE won&amp;rsquo ; t be caught up in the conflagration . Oh , and where the first XFL played up the sex appeal of cheerleaders &amp;mdash ; NBC&amp;rsquo ; s own Bob Costas called it a cross between high-school football and a &amp;ldquo ; tawdry strip club&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; this one , McMahon says , will be &amp;ldquo ; family friendly . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is all very puzzling . Asked a few different ways why he thought there was a market for a new football league today when there clearly was not one before , McMahon offered variations of &amp;ldquo ; people like football&amp;rdquo ; and &amp;ldquo ; there are months when there is no football . &amp;rdquo ; I am fairly certain these conditions existed in 2001 . It is true that , as McMahon said , the media landscape is vastly different now and an upstart league wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily need a big network TV deal to survive , but it will need some way to make money from viewership . How eager would Amazon or Facebook be to buy up the streaming rights of the successor to a football league that McMahon himself once called a &amp;ldquo ; colossal failure&amp;rdquo ; ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A conspiracy theorist might imagine that , despite McMahon&amp;rsquo ; s milquetoast pronouncements on Thursday , he really does intend the new XFL to be everything the NFL is not . More head shots , no late hits , no anthem kneeling and maybe every once in a while someone has to wrestle a bear . Part of the reason for the long runway before a game is played , he said , is so the XFL can hear from fans about what they want . Does he hope that middle America will tell him what he wants to hear ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It sounds , admittedly , like a crazy plan . " data-portal-copyright= " Maddie Meyer " **28;14508;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77099887 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It might have been nice to see two teams compete in the Super Bowl who had never won the big game before . Alas , the Evil Empire defeated the Jacksonville Jaguars 24-20 on Sunday to advance to the title game for the 10th time , nearly one-fifth of the 52 Super Bowls . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the elements of New England&amp;rsquo ; s come-from-behind victory was that they were outgained by Jacksonville 374 yards to 344 . The Jaguars defence was stout , as promised , but couldn&amp;rsquo ; t hold back Tom Brady and the Patriots offence after Blake Bortles and his offence stopped scoring . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Is it unusual for the Super Bowl participants to be outgained by the opponent in their conference championship game victory ? It turns out the answer is no . And for the Patriots it&amp;rsquo ; s even less unusual . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below is a chart of the teams who won every conference championship game in the post-merger era , plotting their yards gained against yards allowed . New England&amp;rsquo ; s victories are noted in blue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;14538;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;14573;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It happens that 30 of the 96 conference champions were outgained by their opponent , ranging from the minus-2 differential for the 2010 Steelers to minus-218 for the 2009 Saints . Seventeen teams have been outgained by 50 or more yards and four by 100 or more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Patriots have split their 10 AFC title games evenly . The four previous Patriots team with a negative differential split their Super Bowls 2-2 . New England is 3-2 in the five Super Bowls in which they had a positive differential in the conference title game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Philadelphia Eagles have had a positive differential of more than 100 yards in all three of their winning conference title games and lost their two previous Super Bowls . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are no omens lurking here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Photo of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; No. 1 seeds and 13-3 teams are rarely considered underdogs in the NFL playoffs , but the Eagles have widely been viewed as such ever since Carson Wentz tore his ACL in Week 14 .
