More impressively , the NHL reports it is the first time Calgary has swept a road trip of at least four games in more than 10 years . The Flames start their five-day break today and play next on Saturday afternoon at home against Winnipeg . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Denis Shapovalov won his first-round match at the Australian Open overnight , but the opening day of play in Melbourne was replete with upsets . No. 8 men&amp;rsquo ; s seed Jack Sock , No. 11 Kevin Anderson , No. 16 John Isner and No. 18 Lucas Pouille all lost , just as No.5 Venus Williams , No. 10 Coco Vandeweghe and No. 13 Sloane Stephens did on the women&amp;rsquo ; s side . In all , eight of nine American women ( and three of five men ) were eliminated , decimating the country&amp;rsquo ; s interests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First-round play resumes at 7 p.m . ET . The Canadians in action tonight are Milos Raonic ( vs . Lukas Lacko ) , Peter Polansky ( vs . Karen Khachanov ) and Eugenie Bouchard ( vs . Oceane Dodin ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;15261;TOOLONG ... title= " Denis Shapovalov celebrates a point against Stefanos Tsitsipas at the Australian Open on Jan. 15. " alt= " Denis Shapovalov celebrates a point against Stefanos Tsitsipas at the Australian Open on Jan. 15. " data-portal-copyright= " Michael Dodge " **28;15296;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77084284 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Kevin Glenn&amp;rsquo ; s tour of the CFL is now complete . The 38-year-old quarterback has signed with Edmonton , the only team he had yet to play for . The Detroit native has been in the league since 2001 ; he helped lead Saskatchewan to the East final this season as a crossover team . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Mike Mularkey&amp;rsquo ; s first playoff win as head coach of the Titans was also his last . Mularkey and the team agreed to part ways after failing to align on a cohesive vision for the future , controlling owner Amy Adams Strunk said in a statement this morning . Tennessee will move forward without the coach who led them back to the post-season for the first time since 2008 . The Titans snagged an AFC wild-card berth at 9-7 this season and beat the Chiefs 22-21 in the first round , before stumbling to a 35-14 loss to New England on Saturday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The Blue Jays plan to lengthen the protective netting that runs parallel to the baselines at the Rogers Centre to the outfield end of each dugout , the team said this morning . They also plan to replace the netting behind home plate and extend its height by three metres . The Jays&amp;rsquo ; statement said the new &amp;ldquo ; **26;15326;TOOLONG ; netting will blend into the background to provide better sight lines and added that the new specifications exceed MLB&amp;rsquo ; s recommended safety guidelines . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Several teams have pledged to expand netting at their home parks in the wake of a frightening incident at Yankee Stadium in September , when a young girl sitting behind the third-base dugout was struck in the face by a 105-mph line drive less than a second after it left Todd Frazier&amp;rsquo ; s bat . The Yankees announced plans to extend their baseline netting last week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; CBC announced today it has signed a deal with Corus&amp;rsquo ; s Telelatino Network to offer select portions of the 2018 and 2020 Olympics in Spanish and Italian . For Pyeongchang , TLN will show one hockey game a day ( men&amp;rsquo ; s and women&amp;rsquo ; s ) in both languages , as well as primetime encore broadcasts and highlights . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;15354;TOOLONG ... title= " Minnesota Vikings quarterback Case Keenum celebrates a playoff win over the New Orleans Saints on Jan. 14. " alt= " Minnesota Vikings quarterback Case Keenum celebrates a playoff win over the New Orleans Saints on Jan. 14. " data-portal-copyright= " Jamie Squire " **28;15389;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77084271 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Social media was still buzzing this morning about the Vikings&amp;rsquo ; astonishing and unlikely last-play victory over the Saints on Sunday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The win allowed Minnesota to become the first team to advance past the division round for a chance to play the Super Bowl in the stadium where they play their regular-season home games . This weekend , the Vikings will travel to Philadelphia , where a win against the Eagles will mean playing Super Bowl LII back home at US Bank Stadium on Feb. 4. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It seemed worth looking into the circumstances around the first seven attempts . But it didn&amp;rsquo ; t take long to realize the whole story was riding the thin edge of a strict definition of what &amp;ldquo ; home&amp;rdquo ; means . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The whole narrative falls apart while looking at Super Bowl XI , the game played at the Rose Bowl in Pasadena , Calif. , to decide the champion of the 1976 NFL season . It turns out that maybe there were actually 12 teams with a chance to play the Super Bowl at home , if &amp;ldquo ; home&amp;rdquo ; means home market rather than home stadium . And three of those extra four teams not only got to the conference championship , two went to the Super Bowl and one of them won . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The NFL and broadcasters are pressing a strict definition of &amp;ldquo ; home&amp;rdquo ; meaning home stadium to add drama to Minnesota&amp;rsquo ; s push . And really , the whole thing is sort of dumb since most NFL franchises have never had the opportunity to host a Super Bowl , leaving this distinction to be fought over primarily by a handful of teams in the south and west and teams like Minnesota with domes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below is a map showing the locations of the eight teams that had a chance to play the Super Bowl in their home stadium ( indicated by the numbers ) and the four who had a chance to play in a stadium within their home market ( indicated by the letters ) . Below the map are annotation for each number and letter . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;15419;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;15454;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1,2 : Miami Dolphins ( Super Bowl V and XIII ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Super Bowl V , to conclude the 1970 season , was already the third Super Bowl to be played at the Orange Bowl in Miami . But it was the first time the Dolphins had made the playoffs after four fruitless seasons in the AFL before the merger with the NFL . It was legendary Dolphins coach Don Shula&amp;rsquo ; s first season in Miami after seven season in Baltimore . But Miami , which finished 10-4 in the regular season and earned the AFC wild-card spot , fell 21-14 against Oakland , which was 8-4-2 but earned the home game because it was a division winner . Shula&amp;rsquo ; s former team , the Colts , went on to win the Super Bowl over Dallas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Dolphins didn&amp;rsquo ; t make the playoffs the next time the Super Bowl was at the Orange Bowl , following the 1975 season , but they rose to the occasion the next time , following the 1978 season . However , Miami didn&amp;rsquo ; t make it past the wild-card round despite an 11-5 record . They turned the ball over five times and lost 17-9 at home to the Houston Oilers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3,4 : Miami Dolphins ( Super Bowl XXIX and XXXIII ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The next opportunity for a home-stadium Super Bowl didn&amp;rsquo ; t come until 16 years later , again with the Dolphins . Now playing at Joe Robbie Stadium in Miami Gardens , Fla. , the Dolphins made it to the division round following the 1994 season but blew a 21-6 halftime lead in San Diego to lose 22-21. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Four years after that , the Dolphins had their final opportunity . Miami was only a wild-card team and , after getting past Buffalo in the first round , was destroyed 38-3 in Denver as Terrell Davis ran for 199 yards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 : Tampa Bay Buccaneers ( Super Bowl XXXV ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This was the third Super Bowl in Tampa but the first at Raymond James Stadium . The 2000 season was the middle of just the second period of sustained success for the mostly sad-sack Bucs , who had now made the playoffs in three of four seasons for head coach Tony Dungy . But after making the conference final the season before , Tampa Bay was held to only 199 yards of offence in a 21-3 loss at Philadelphia in the wild-card round . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;6 : Arizona Cardinals ( Super Bowl XLIX ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was another 14 seasons before the Cardinals had the next chance . It took an 11-5 record to earn a wild-card berth in the 2014 season , yet the Cardinals had to travel to Carolina to play the Panthers , who were a division winner with a 7-8-1 record . Arizona had a 14-13 halftime lead but was shut out in the second half , save for a meaningless safety Carolina conceded with three seconds left . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;7 : Houston Texans ( Super Bowl LI ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last season , Houston was getting its first Super Bowl in 13 years and the Texans made the playoffs for the second straight season . After beating Oakland in the wild-card round , Houston had to travel to New England for the division round . They trailed the Patriots 17-13 at halftime but were unable to score more than a field goal in the second half , and lost 34-16 . New England travelled to Houston three weeks later to stage the greatest comeback victory in Super Bowl history . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;8 : Minnesota Vikings ( Super Bowl LII ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;blockquote class= " twitter-tweet " data-width= " 550 " data-dnt= " true " &gt;&lt;p lang= " en " dir= " ltr " &gt; ? ? &lt;a href= " https : **33;15484;TOOLONG ... Sideline Cam&lt;a href= " https : **33;15519;TOOLONG ... &lt;a href= " https : //t.co/fRe9CZTlwt " &gt;pic.twitter ... -- Minnesota Vikings ( @Vikings ) &lt;a href= " https : **33;15554;TOOLONG ... 15 , **33;15589;TOOLONG &lt;script async src= " https : **33;15624;TOOLONG " charset= " utf-8 " &gt;&lt;/script&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A , B , D : Los Angeles Rams ( Super Bowl XI , XIV and XXI ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Verison 1.0 of the L.A. Rams made the playoffs in three of the five seasons the Super Bowl was played at the Rose Bowl in nearby Pasadena , Calif . The Rams played at the L.A. Coliseum through 1979 and were out in Anaheim from 1980-94 while keeping the Los Angeles affiliation . The Rose Bowl is only 23 kilometres from the Coliseum . New Era Field , home of the Bills , is nearly 20 kilometres from downtown Buffalo . You can easily drive a 23-kilometre straight line and never leave the city of Calgary . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 1976 Rams made the conference championship game , losing 24-13 to Minnesota . The 1986 Rams didn&amp;rsquo ; t make it out of the wild-card round , losing to Washington 19-7 . But the 1979 Rams made it to the Super Bowl , where they lost 31-19 to Pittsburgh , the cap to the Steelers&amp;rsquo ; four Super Bowl victories in six seasons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;C : San Francisco 49ers ( Super Bowl XIX ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The only Super Bowl played at Stanford Stadium in Palo Alto , Calif. , came near the beginning of the Bill Walsh-Joe Montana coach-quarterback pairing that produced seven playoff appearances in eight seasons and three Super Bowl titles . The 1984 49ers finished the season 15-1 , losing only in Pittsburgh in Week 7 , and rolled to the title game allowing only 10 points to the Giants in the division round and shutting out the Bears in the conference final . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the Super Bowl &amp;mdash ; played about 50 kilometres from their home stadium at Candlestick Park , but well within the Bay Area &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; the 49ers earned their second rings with a 38-16 win over Miami . It wasn&amp;rsquo ; t in their home stadium , but it&amp;rsquo ; s now been 33 years since an NFL team won a Super Bowl in their home market . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Photos of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The making of a miracle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;15659;TOOLONG ... title= " Minnesota Vikings receiver Stefon Diggs hauls in the winning pass against the New Orleans Saints on Jan. 14. Hope Kyle gets better . &lt;a href= " https : //t.co/KcA7ZyEENw " &gt;pic.twitter ... -- De52 ( @BasedMasai ) &lt;a href= " https : **33;16516;TOOLONG ... 9 , **33;16551;TOOLONG &lt;script async src= " https : **33;16586;TOOLONG " charset= " utf-8 " &gt;&lt;/script&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; At the midpoint of the season , the NHL reports that the Lightning are on track to become only the second team in history to have the league&amp;rsquo ; s best record after missing the playoffs the previous season . The 1993-94 New York Rangers also did the trick &amp;mdash ; and followed up with a Stanley Cup victory . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With scoring up in the league , eight players have already reached 50 points , which is the most at the halfway mark since 10 players in 2007-08 . In addition , 24 players who have played a minimum of 20 games are averaging a point a game . The NHL says the last season in which 24 players finished the year at a point a game was 2006-07 , when 32 reached that plateau . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Several Canadians will take the court tonight in the opening round of qualifying at the Australian Open . " alt= " Vasek Pospisil plays a forehand to David Goffin at the Hopman Cup in Perth , Australia , on Jan. 5. " data-portal-copyright= " Paul Kane " **28;16656;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77072161 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The Tiger-Cats have until Jan. 31 to present Johnny Manziel with a &amp;ldquo ; fair&amp;rdquo ; contract offer , Manziel&amp;rsquo ; s agent said in a statement last night . Erik Burkhardt says he and Manziel , the 2012 Heisman Trophy winner and failed Browns quarterback , plan to negotiate exclusively with the Ticats until that date , rather than attempt to persuade them to trade his rights to another CFL team . Fair , to Burkhardt , would be an offer on par with the league-leading $520,000 Hamilton paid to Zach Collaros in 2017. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; IOC president Thomas Bach has heralded North Korea&amp;rsquo ; s decision to send athletes , officials , spectators and journalists to the Olympics in South Korea as &amp;ldquo ; a great step forward in the Olympic spirit . &amp;rdquo ; The North Koreans agreed to take part in the Pyeongchang Games during a rare meeting with the South today . Ryom Tae Ok and Kim Ju Sik , a figure skating pair who finished 15th at the world championships last year , are the only North Koreans to qualify for Pyeongchang so far , though the IOC could grant wild-card spots to other athletes to fortify the country&amp;rsquo ; s delegation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; CBC has revealed the broadcasters it will send to the Olympics &amp;mdash ; a group that includes , of all people , former Senators star and malcontent Alexei Yashin , who joins Kate Beirness , Cassie Campbell-Pascall , Ray Ferraro and others on the network&amp;rsquo ; s hockey lineup . Chris Cuthbert and Mark Lee will call the hockey games , while Scott Russell returns as the face of primetime coverage and Steve Armitage handles long track speed skating play-by-play at his 16th Olympics . The full list of commentators , analysts and reporters can be found &lt;a href= " http : **34;16686;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;here&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;16722;TOOLONG ... title= " In this Aug. 13 , 2012 file photo , NFL broadcaster Jon Gruden greets Raiders fans before a pre-season game in Oakland . " alt= " In this Aug. 13 , 2012 file photo , NFL broadcaster Jon Gruden greets Raiders fans before a pre-season game in Oakland . " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Avelar " **28;16757;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77072162 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At 3 p.m . ET , the Oakland Raiders will hold a press conference to introduce Jon Gruden as their next head coach . The most interesting part of the deal isn&amp;rsquo ; t that Gruden finally chose to leave ESPN&amp;rsquo ; s Monday Night Football booth after nine years , but that the Raiders were willing to make an unprecedented contract offer to entice him : 10 years and US$100 million , reportedly . Almost everyone who has voiced an opinion about the length and heft of the deal has been incredulous . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;16787;TOOLONG ... of Jon Gruden 's press **42;16823;TOOLONG &l; ... It seemed worth investigating how likely it is Gruden could finish the whole 10 years . There have been a few decades-long head coaching tenures in the NFL , but they are rare . How often has one head coach lasted 10 or more years with one team in one continuous stretch ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below is a histogram showing the number and the percentage of head coaches broken into two-year increments , regardless of the number of teams he&amp;rsquo ; s worked for . The first bar shows the head coaches who have been the sideline boss for a total of 1-2 years , then 3-4 years , 5-6 years , and so on up to George Halas&amp;rsquo ; s 40 years with the Bears ( of whom he also happened to be the owner ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;16867;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16902;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Only 13.9 per cent of the 489 head coaches in the AFL and NFL have lasted 10 years or more . Gruden already has 11 years under his belt , four with the Raiders from 1998-2001 and seven with the Buccaneers from 2002-08 . He&amp;rsquo ; s already beaten those odds . To become a 20-year man , he would join a list of only 15 men that includes Don Shula and Curly Lambeau ( 33 years ) , Tom Landry ( 29 years ) , Bill Belichick ( 23 years and counting ) , and Chuck Noll and Dan Reeves ( 23 years ) . Andy Reid will hit 20 years next season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of the 68 head coaches who have had careers of 10 or more years , only 33 managed to have a tenure with one one team that lasted for 10 or more years . Below is a chart of those tenures , sorted from earliest to most recent . Incredibly , there are six coaches currently compiling one of these long-term assignments . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;16932;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16967;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We&amp;rsquo ; re including the Saints&amp;rsquo ; Sean Payton , even though his tenure is bisected by his 2012 suspension from the Bountygate investigation . We won&amp;rsquo ; t credit him for the year he sat out , but we&amp;rsquo ; ll give him credit for an unbroken term of employment . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s interesting that Halas was head coach for 40 seasons , but that they came in four 10-year increments . He was a owner-player-coach in the first segment , and he returned the bench for the second instalment to save money during the Depression . His third stint came after he served in the Navy . His fourth period lasted until the beginning of the Super Bowl era. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , let&amp;rsquo ; s look at the Raiders&amp;rsquo ; history , with a chart that shows their wins each season , labels the three Super Bowl-winning seasons and marks the four periods in which the head coach lasted at least as long as Gruden&amp;rsquo ; s first four-year tenure . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;16997;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;17032;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; John Madden and Tom Flores are easily the two most successful coaches in team history , which spans 21 head coaches . The Raiders have only been to the playoffs twice since Gruden was traded by Al Davis to Tampa Bay before the 2002 season for four draft picks and $8 million . Oakland , under Bill Callahan , lost Super Bowl XXXVII to Gruden and the Buccaneers that first season after the trade . After a 13-year drought , the Raiders were a wild-card team in 2016 under Jack Del Rio . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now Gruden has been given a long horizon to return to Raiders to their glory of the late &amp;rsquo ; 70s and early &amp;rsquo ; 80s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Photo of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jalen Hurts was struggling . The sophomore quarterback had only lost twice in his two years at Alabama , and he&amp;rsquo ; d led the Crimson Tide to a 24-6 suppression of Clemson in the Jan. 1 Sugar Bowl . But he&amp;rsquo ; d completed just three passes for 21 yards by halftime of the national title game , and so when Alabama returned to the field down 13-0 to Georgia , head coach Nick Saban kept him on the sideline. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Instead , Saban turned to Tua Tagovailoa , the true freshman from Hawaii who spearheaded the Tide&amp;rsquo ; s stirring second-half comeback and 26-23 overtime win in Atlanta last night . Tagovailoa hit 14 of 24 passes for 166 yards and three touchdowns , none bigger than a 41-yard strike to DeVonta Smith on the game&amp;rsquo ; s final play . The result was Alabama&amp;rsquo ; s fifth national championship in the last nine years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;17062;TOOLONG ... title= " Alabama quarterback Tua Tagovailoa ( centre ) celebrates his team 's win over Georgia in the NCAA football championship game in Atlanta on Jan. 8. Ebenhack/The Associated Press " **28;17512;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77068733 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; JACKSONVILLE , Fla. &amp;mdash ; What happens when both quarterbacks in an NFL playoff game stink to high heaven as passers ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One team beats the other 10-3 , in a hellish game to watch . It happened Sunday at EverBank Field , when the host Jacksonville Jaguars eked past the Buffalo Bills in the second AFC wild-card game of the weekend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ugly ? Odoriferous ? Sure . But so what , said Jaguars cornerback Jalen Ramsey , whose interception near mid-field with 26 seconds left sealed the Jaguars&amp;rsquo ; first playoff victory in exactly 10 years , less two days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Yeah , I don&amp;rsquo ; t care what people call it , &amp;rdquo ; Ramsey said . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re going to come in here for another week , while other teams are sitting home . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; True enough . Twenty-four teams , to be exact . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , oh , that woeful quarterbacking on Sunday . Jacksonville&amp;rsquo ; s Blake Bortles was so awful , aerially , he was able to complete a pass to only one wide receiver all day &amp;mdash ; backup Dede Westbrook . He had five catches for 48 yards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bortles actually rushed for more yards ( 88 ) on impromptu scrambles than he passed for ( 87 , on 12-of-23 throwing ) . Time and again in the first half , Bortles badly missed simple short throws , mostly screens . Perhaps that&amp;rsquo ; s why in the second half he chose to scramble so often &amp;mdash ; eight times for 53 yards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He&amp;rsquo ; s an athlete , that&amp;rsquo ; s what that tells me , &amp;rdquo ; Jaguars left tackle Cam Robinson said . &amp;ldquo ; He&amp;rsquo ; s going to go out there and do whatever it takes for us to win . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bortles&amp;rsquo ; scrambles proved the difference , because the Bills defence successfully blanketed the Jags&amp;rsquo ; sensational rookie running back Leonard Fournette , holding him to just 57 yards on 21 carries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bortles scrambled three times for 20 yards on the game&amp;rsquo ; s only ( and decisive ) touchdown drive late in the third quarter , a march that covered 86 yards in 15 plays and chewed 8:52 off the clock . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Those quarterback runs really hurt us , &amp;rdquo ; Bills linebacker Preston Brown said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bortles capped the drive with a 4th-and-goal play-action TD pass from the Buffalo one-yard line , to backup tight end Ben Koyack. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thereafter , the Bills offence never advanced further than Jacksonville&amp;rsquo ; s 38. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The defence came back and did a heck of a job in the second half , &amp;rdquo ; Jaguars head coach Doug Marrone said . &amp;ldquo ; Playoff football is about you just going out there to win . No one is trying to win a beauty contest . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Noted . But that said . . . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; ( Offensively ) , we never really got into a rhythm and we weren&amp;rsquo ; t able to get anything on third downs , so I&amp;rsquo ; d be a fool to sit here and say I&amp;rsquo ; m not concerned , &amp;rdquo ; said Marrone. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bills quarterback Tyrod Taylor hit enough third-down throws in the first half to pass for 90 yards by halftime , when the scored was tied 3-3 . But he was dreadful thereafter , completing 6-of-16 for 44 yards until getting knocked out ( perhaps literally , briefly ) on a wicked but legal hit with 1:17 left . That came on Buffalo&amp;rsquo ; s last , desperation drive to try to tie or win the game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Backup Bills QB Nathan Peterman finished up , and although he scrambled for a drive-extending first down on 4th-and-3 , then hit receiver Deonte Thompson for 14 yards on a deepish curl to the Jaguars&amp;rsquo ; 38 , he was flagged for intentional grounding , then threw the hope-ending pick . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But this one&amp;rsquo ; s on Taylor . Significantly , it was Buffalo&amp;rsquo ; s fourth loss this season in which Taylor failed to pass for as many as 45 yards in the second half , after Cincinnati ( 39 ) , New Orleans ( eight ) and the first New England game ( 17 ) . That&amp;rsquo ; s unacceptably bad quarterbacking at the NFL level , when your team needs you most . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Will the Bills bring him back ? Odds seem against it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for now , Taylor has entered concussion protocol . Thankfully , he did not appear overly pained or affected while sitting on a stool at his locker afterward . That&amp;rsquo ; s hopefully an indication his concussion is not a long-term one . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bills head coach Sean McDermott was gracious in talking about Taylor afterward , preferring to focus on his character , rather than his passing &amp;ndash ; never a good thing , really , if you&amp;rsquo ; re the QB . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I thought he handled himself just like the rest of our football team , &amp;rdquo ; McDermott said , &amp;ldquo ; with class and with a lot of pride . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jacksonville next plays the Steelers in Pittsburgh &amp;ndash ; for the second time this season &amp;ndash ; on Sunday afternoon at 1:05 p.m . " data-portal-copyright= " Maddie Meyer " **28;18027;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77047724 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Jurassic World&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The reinvention of the Toronto Raptors seemed like a fine idea that would be tough to execute . The biggest hurdle : how would an offence that was almost entirely built around two ball-dominant veterans manage to open up ? And how would DeMar DeRozan and Kyle Lowry handle the change ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The answer so far is just fine , thanks . But will they be able to stick to the ( new ) plan in the playoffs , and will that be enough to get them back to the East final again ? We&amp;rsquo ; ll say yes , but it won&amp;rsquo ; t quite get them past LeBron and the Cavaliers . Still , an early playoff series in which the Raptors do not struggle to defeat a much lower seed would be a marked improvement . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Decision III&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whatever happens with LeBron and the Cavaliers in the post-season , the NBA will come to a complete stop next summer as the league waits to see whether he will again switch teams , a move that could this time throw both conferences for a loop . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I remain a LeBron-to-the-Lakers skeptic , in part because I can&amp;rsquo ; t imagine him ripping Cleveland&amp;rsquo ; s heart out again and in part because the Lakers are not good . Does he really want to drag that young team into a low playoff seed in the West and then get caved in by Golden State or Houston in the first round ? It seems like a weird final act for his career . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;18057;TOOLONG ... title= " Ottawa Senators defenceman Erik Karlsson skates against the Montreal Canadiens in the NHL 100 Classic on Dec. 16. " alt= " Ottawa Senators defenceman Erik Karlsson skates against the Montreal Canadiens in the NHL 100 Classic on Dec. 16. " data-portal-copyright= " Jana Chytilova " **28;18092;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77047730 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Other Decision&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Erik Karlsson is under contract through the 2018-19 season , but if the Ottawa Senators decide they can not afford a deal in the $10-million-per-year range , then Karlsson would immediately become the biggest trade chip in recent memory . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Aside from the seeming lunacy of trading Karlsson &amp;mdash ; why are you in the NHL business if your team can not pay fair value for an elite player ? &amp;mdash ; there is the complicating factor that the Senators are presently negotiating the potential terms for a new downtown arena . They will be expecting some degree of public contribution to the project , whether in land , tax breaks , or straight cash . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That will be a tough request , but it will be hilariously tough if it comes as the team is unloading its franchise player . Good thing owner Eugene Melnyk has all that public goodwill to fall back on . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Coffee pucks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The news that the NHL has set the expansion fee for a potential team in Seattle at US$650 million was treated in many corners as though it was almost a done deal . But the coming months will make clear whether the group headed by former MLSE boss Tim Leiweke is actually going to go through with it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That organization has already committed $600 million to the renovation of KeyArena , and it has long been thought that the NBA was the main target for a tenant . Will they come up with all that cash for a secondary renter ? At the least , the NHL might want the Seattle group to hold off on expansion so it can use their plans to try to blackmail more public money out of the Calgary Flames . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;18122;TOOLONG ... title= " Barcelona forward Lionel Messi celebrates his goal against Real Madrid on Dec. 23. " alt= " Barcelona forward Lionel Messi celebrates his goal against Real Madrid on Dec. 23. " data-portal-copyright= " Curto De La Torre " **28;18157;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77047734 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The red carpet&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Russia will host the World Cup next summer , and if it is true that the one thing sports fans hate above all else is travel complaints on social media from writers , then watch out . Russia is , you know , huge . The soccer venues range from St. Petersburg to Sochi and from Kaliningrad to Yekaterinburg . ( Note : Actual places . The trip would take 39 hours by car . ) At least they confined it to the European part of Russia , and didn&amp;rsquo ; t include the Asian part . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , we will take Argentina to win in Lionel Messi&amp;rsquo ; s farewell tournament , because that would be fun . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Roar or whimper ? &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tiger Woods is , presumably , going to continue his latest comeback , and all he has to do is get through February without surgery to beat the length of his previous comeback . But will he actually contend ? At a real tournament ? Or even at a major ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Predictions on this one are pointless because of the uncertainty of his health , but there is also this : the Woods of old could scare opponents into mistakes just by whipping out his resume . No one fears him now . How will Tiger Woods compete when he is just a guy ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;18187;TOOLONG ... title= " Tiger Woods ' bag is shown during the pro-am event at the Hero World Challenge in Nassau , Bahamas , on Nov. 29. " data-portal-copyright= " Mike Ehrmann " **28;18222;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 76993050 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Return of El Shapo&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tennis tends to fall off the sporting map after the U.S. Open , and so it might surprise some to learn that after his stirring summer that included an appearance in the final in Montreal and three wins in New York , Denis Shapovalov was 2-6 through October and November . But the Canadian teen is still ranked 51st in the world and will return to the big stage at the Australian Open , which begins Jan. 15 in Melbourne . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Will he be able to regain the form that saw him upset multiple major champions late last season ? And will he have found a cap that properly fits his head ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;No bombs , please&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Olympics begin in Pyeongchang , South Korea , in February , and there are several interesting storylines . How many Russians will compete for Team Generic ? Will Team Canada be able to repeat its historic showing from Sochi and finish around the top of the medal table ? Will anyone be able to identify more than three players on the Canadian men&amp;rsquo ; s hockey roster ? And will Donald Trump and Kim Jong-Un stop taunting each other long enough to let the Games happen without incident ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Short answers to the above : Many , yes , no and I sure hope so . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;18252;TOOLONG ... title= " Toronto Blue Jays GM Ross Atkins speaks to reporters at a season-ending press conference on Oct. 3. " data-portal-copyright= " Michael Peake " **28;18287;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 76777403 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Surface this&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Let us review the past quarter century of the Toronto Blue Jays : 22 seasons of such also-ran status that fans would say the phrase &amp;ldquo ; meaningful September baseball&amp;rdquo ; with a mixture of longing and wonder , then two seasons of glory , and then back to hopelessness again . The team either makes the ALCS or can&amp;rsquo ; t even make it through August with anything approaching hope . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Management seems disinclined to tear anything down , so the hope for fans this time is that the veteran-laden team can avoid the disastrous April of last year and ride better health to something like the fringes of the AL wild-card race . It could happen , but they really need to sign some guys first . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , ownership is either fixing to sell the team or is maybe considering it , or is totally not planning to , depending on the report . We do know they want to &amp;ldquo ; surface value&amp;rdquo ; for the Blue Jays on their balance sheet , which would make for a pretty lousy hashtag , frankly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;18317;TOOLONG ... a 28-3 Super Bowl choke to two Toronto championships , sports were kind of nuts in **36;18353;TOOLONG &lt;h3 &g; ... Lions , Bears decide to start the new year with new head coacheshttp : **33;18603;TOOLONG ... src= " https : **33;18638;TOOLONG ... title= " Detroit Lions head coach Jim Caldwell kicked off Monday 's purge of NFL coaches when he was let go despite a 9-7 season . " alt= " Detroit Lions head coach Jim Caldwell kicked off Monday 's purge of NFL coaches when he was let go despite a 9-7 season . " data-portal-copyright= " George Bridges " **28;18673;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77056046 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; John Fox and Jim Caldwell were the latest head coaches to go as the firing-and-hiring season for NFL head coaches began in earnest with the close of the regular season . And , by midday Monday , Bruce Arians was stepping down as coach of the Cardinals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Chicago Bears parted with Fox after a 5-11 season and the Lions dropped Caldwell after a 9-7 finish , marking two changes in the NFC North&amp;rsquo ; s four teams . Those firings came early on New Year&amp;rsquo ; s Day and followed the dismissals of Jack Del Rio by the Raiders and Chuck Pagano by the Colts on New Year&amp;rsquo ; s Eve . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Arians , 65 , told members of the Cardinals organization that he would retire as coach , according to a person familiar with the situation , after his fifth season . He previously was the interim coach of the Indianapolis Colts while Pagano battled cancer . The Cardinals went 8-8 this season and were 15-16-1 over the past two seasons after three straight seasons with 10 or more victories . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One NFL coach &amp;mdash ; Ben McAdoo of the New York Giants &amp;mdash ; had already been fired before Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s regular season finales and another , Cincinnati&amp;rsquo ; s Marvin Lewis , remains in limbo despite meeting with owner Mike Brown on Monday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here is a look at all the moves , the likely moves and the top candidates to fill the vacancies . &lt;/p&gt; **28;18703;TOOLONG &lt;p ... &lt;strong&gt;John Fox : &lt;/strong&gt; The record will show that Fox leaves with only 14 victories in 48 games over three seasons . Although Fox had success elsewhere , he could never duplicate that with the Bears , who now may seek an offensive-minded coach rather than someone focused on defence , as Fox , who has one year left on a four-year contract , was . With Mitchell Trubisky at quarterback , a change in philosophy most likely is in order . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Thank you to all the players , coaches , the city of Chicago and Bears fans everywhere , &amp;rdquo ; Fox said in a midmorning statement . &amp;ldquo ; Your passion for the game and this team is unmatched in the NFL . Today is the tough part of our results-oriented business , but I wish the Bears organization the best for years to come . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jim Caldwell : &lt;/strong&gt; A 9-7 finish nonetheless brought the end of Caldwell&amp;rsquo ; s time in Detroit . Like Fox , Caldwell could never duplicate his previous success in four seasons with the Lions , despite the presence of a quarterback like Matthew Stafford . Caldwell , unlike Fox , has had winning seasons in three of his four years with the teams , although the Lions ( 36-28 under Caldwell ) have missed the playoffs twice in the past three years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Have you ever heard me defend or anything ? &amp;rdquo ; Caldwell said Sunday after the Lions swept the Packers for the first time since 1991. &amp;ldquo ; Not to anybody . It&amp;rsquo ; s not just to you . It&amp;rsquo ; s not to anybody . I don&amp;rsquo ; t make any excuses . It&amp;rsquo ; s just not the way I live my life . We go out and we do what we do and let everybody make an assessment from there . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What&amp;rsquo ; s the real assessment is wins , wins and losses . That&amp;rsquo ; s the key . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Caldwell leaves Detroit with the highest win percentage by a Lions head coach in the Super Bowl era. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Lions reportedly have requested permission to speak to Patriots defensive coordinator Matt Patricia , Panthers defensive coordinator Steve Wilks , Vikings offensive coordinator Pat Shurmur and Texans defensive coordinator Mike Vrabel . They&amp;rsquo ; re also expected to interview Caldwell&amp;rsquo ; s defensive coordinator , Teryl Austin . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jack Del Rio : &lt;/strong&gt; Shortly after the Raiders&amp;rsquo ; 30-10 loss to the Los Angeles Chargers , Del Rio stepped to the podium and did something coaches rarely do : he announced that Oakland Raiders owner Mark Davis had told him he was fired . Del Rio , who received a four-year contract extension earlier this year , finished with an 25-23 record as head coach in Oakland &amp;mdash ; with the lion&amp;rsquo ; s share of the losses ( 10 ) coming in 2017. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He told me he loved me and appreciated all I did to get this program going in the right direction , but that he felt the need to change , &amp;rdquo ; an emotional Del Rio said ( via ESPN ) . &amp;ldquo ; I told him how much I appreciated the opportunity he gave me ; very grateful , my childhood team . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But it&amp;rsquo ; s a results business . I understand that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Raiders are expected to quickly move to sign Jon Gruden , who won Super Bowl XXXVII while head coach of the Tampa Bay Buccaneers . Gruden has not coached in the NFL since 2009 when he was fired by the Bucs and has been an analyst with ESPN since then . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Chuck Pagano : &lt;/strong&gt; The first firing of a coach this off-season was unsurprising . The announcement officially came soon after Indianapolis&amp;rsquo ; s 22-13 win Sunday over the Texans , which gave the team a 4-12 record . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Chuck Pagano provided Colts fans with many exciting wins and memories as head coach of the Colts , &amp;rdquo ; Colts owner Jim Irsay said in a statement . &amp;ldquo ; Throughout his tenure in Indianapolis , he impacted the lives of the players he coached , those who he worked with in the organization and Colts fans across the globe . Chuck&amp;rsquo ; s first season was one of the more inspirational stories in NFL history as he courageously battled and overcame leukemia . As a result , his CHUCKSTRONG Foundation has raised millions for cancer research . We are thankful for Chuck&amp;rsquo ; s contributions to our franchise and community and we wish him , Tina and the entire Pagano family nothing but the best moving forward . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In six seasons with the Colts , Pagano compiled a 53-43 record , plus a 3-3 mark in the playoffs , including a loss to the Patriots in the 2015 AFC championship game whence Deflategate emerged . After starting with three straight 11-5 campaigns though , Pagano&amp;rsquo ; s Colts went 8-8 in both 2015 and 2016 , while Andrew Luck was hampered with injuries , including a shoulder problem that required surgery and kept him out of this season altogether . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Expected to be gone&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Marvin Lewis , Bengals : &lt;/strong&gt; There has been speculation that Lewis and the Bengals will agree to part ways . If that happens , Lewis could end up in a front office somewhere , maybe even in Cincinnati . The Bengals could try to land one of Lewis&amp;rsquo ; s former offensive coordinators who left to become head coaches , the Redskins&amp;rsquo ; Jay Gruden or the Browns&amp;rsquo ; Hue Jackson , or they could promote defensive coordinator Paul Guenther . Lewis and owner Mike Brown met Monday morning , but there was no word on whether there had been a resolution of Lewis&amp;rsquo ; s status . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Could go either way&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bill O'Brien , Texans : &lt;/strong&gt; O&amp;rsquo ; Brien , who had three straight 9-7 seasons before this season&amp;rsquo ; s unraveling with injuries to J.J. Watt , Whitney Mercilus and Deshaun Watson , reportedly will be retained . But don&amp;rsquo ; t forget that O&amp;rsquo ; Brien chose Tom Savage , not Watson , to be the Texans&amp;rsquo ; season-opening starter at quarterback. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Hue Jackson , Browns : &lt;/strong&gt; Owner Jimmy Haslam has said that Jackson will be retained for next season . But the Browns are 1-31 since the start of last season . They completed a winless season Sunday . What if John Dorsey , the new GM hired by Haslam , lobbies for a new coach ? The possibility that Cincinnati could be a landing spot for Jackson further complicates any internal politics that might be at work in Cleveland . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jason Garrett , Cowboys : &lt;/strong&gt; Owner Jerry Jones said this week that he&amp;rsquo ; s not considering a coaching change . Will that remain the case following Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s season finale in Philadelphia ? A season that began with seemingly legitimate Super Bowl aspirations ends with the Cowboys missing the playoffs . Jones seems to be directing most of his ire at the league and at NFL Commissioner Roger Goodell for the six-game suspension of Cowboys running back Ezekiel Elliott under the personal conduct policy . But does Garrett deserve some of that blame ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jay Gruden , Redskins : &lt;/strong&gt; Gruden&amp;rsquo ; s contract runs through 2020 , and he did reasonably well this season to keep the Redskins within a game of .500 entering Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s finale against the Giants at MetLife Stadium , which they lost . Gruden probably should stay . But there&amp;rsquo ; s always that &amp;ldquo ; you never know&amp;rdquo ; element at this time of the year , particularly with the Redskins&amp;rsquo ; traditional impatience with coaches . What if the Redskins don&amp;rsquo ; t have to pay the remainder of Gruden&amp;rsquo ; s contract because he lands immediately in Cincinnati ? What if the Bengals were willing to sweeten the deal just a bit by throwing in , say , a draft choice ? Where would Gruden be better off : remaining in Washington , probably without Kirk Cousins at quarterback , or going back to Cincinnati and working with Andy Dalton or A.J. McCarron at quarterback , with Guenther as his defensive coordinator and maybe with Lewis in the front office ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;A vote of confidence&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Vance Joseph , Broncos : &lt;/strong&gt; It won&amp;rsquo ; t be one-and-done for Joseph , even though it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t a promising sign when front office executive John Elway called the team soft . It has been a swift descent for the Broncos in the two seasons since they won the Super Bowl in Peyton Manning&amp;rsquo ; s farewell , and Elway clearly deserves his share of the blame for failing to put a quarterback in place to succeed Manning . But Elway wasn&amp;rsquo ; t going to fire himself and , on Monday morning , he said he isn&amp;rsquo ; t going to fire Joseph , either . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Vance and I had a great talk this morning about our plan to attack this offseason and get better as a team , &amp;rdquo ; Elway tweeted. &amp;ldquo ; We believe in Vance as our head coach . Together , we&amp;rsquo ; ll put in the work to improve in all areas and win in 2018. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Should be fine , unless ... &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Mike Mularkey , Titans : &lt;/strong&gt; All seemed well with the Titans&amp;rsquo ; rebuilding plan until they lost three straight games to put their playoff chances in peril . Quarterback Marcus Mariota has taken a step backward in his third season with just 12 touchdown passes to go along with 15 interceptions . But the Titans got into the AFC playoffs on Sunday with a win over Jacksonville . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;John Harbaugh , Ravens : &lt;/strong&gt; A 31-27 defeat to the Bengals on Sunday and a third straight non-playoff season , particularly under such disappointing circumstances , could turn up the heat on Harbaugh and quarterback Joe Flacco. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The coaches who get to decide for themselves&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Pete Carroll , Seahawks : &lt;/strong&gt; The Seahawks won in improbable fashion last week in Dallas , with more penalty yards than total offense , in a playoff elimination game and still have postseason possibilities this weekend if they beat the Cardinals and the Falcons lose to the Panthers . Either way , the Seahawks are likely to undergo an offseason roster retooling that could involve defensive standouts Richard Sherman , Kam Chancellor , Earl Thomas , Cliff Avril and Michael Bennett . I ain&amp;rsquo ; t old enough to think about retiring ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Not going anywhere&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Todd Bowles , Jets : &lt;/strong&gt; The Jets&amp;rsquo ; five victories are roughly five more than many observers expected them to manage this season , and that apparently was plenty good enough : On Friday , Bowles and General Manager Mike Maccagnan had their contracts extended , the Jets announced . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Dirk Koetter , Buccaneers : &lt;/strong&gt; The Buccaneers are among the league&amp;rsquo ; s most disappointing teams , and it has been reported that Koetter and quarterback Jameis Winston have a strained relationship . I had a column in which the Patriots lost , and I had one in which they staged a historic comeback . I had no version in which they staged a heroic comeback &amp;mdash ; and then lost . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19046;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1353655 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Masters&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; This whole Year in Review will not be about columns I wrote , but this item will , sort of . It was Wednesday of Masters week , and I had just filed &lt;a href= " http : **34;19076;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;a piece on Dustin Johnson&lt;/a&gt; , who came to Augusta with three wins already and whose opponents were saying there was him and there was everyone else . Then Johnson slipped on a staircase at his rental home and hurt his back . Awkward . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Johnson ultimately withdrew , but the tournament didn&amp;rsquo ; t lack for drama . On Sunday , Sergio Garcia packed a microcosm of his career into his final round . He grabbed the lead , then coughed it up and then seized it back with a birdie on 14 and a spectacular eagle on 15 . Then , very Sergio-like , he missed short putts on 16 and 18 , the latter of which was for the win . His majors curse lived . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , a last surprise : in the playoff with Justin Rose , it was the Englishman who bogeyed and Garcia finally had his major victory . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s been such a long time coming , &amp;rdquo ; said Garcia , 37 . He&amp;rsquo ; s got that right . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;El Chapeau&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; It wasn&amp;rsquo ; t that long ago that there was a good debate to be had over which Canadian was more likely to win the country&amp;rsquo ; s first singles tennis major title , Milos Raonic or Eugenie Bouchard . All of a sudden , that mantle might have shifted to a kid very few sports fans had even heard of at this time last year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Denis Shapovalov , gangly teen from the Toronto suburbs who was a junior star but had not played much on the top senior circuit , blew into the summer hard court season and beat three top-10 players , including an in-form Rafael freaking Nadal . He made it to the final in Montreal and through three rounds at the U.S. Open , earning comparisons to a young Nadal and a young Roger Federer ( ! ) , while people were still trying to figure out how to say his name . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s pretty intimidating on paper , &amp;ldquo ; he said , encouraging all to go with Shapo , the new nickname . Between that and the signature backwards hat , he won&amp;rsquo ; t sneak up on anyone this season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;19112;TOOLONG ... title= " Toronto Blue Jays 3B Josh Donaldson walks off the field after being left on base against the Baltimore Orioles on Sept. 3. " alt= " Toronto Blue Jays 3B Josh Donaldson walks off the field after being left on base against the Baltimore Orioles on Sept. 3. " data-portal-copyright= " Gail Burton " **28;19147;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 76436104 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Blew Jays&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canada&amp;rsquo ; s lone baseball team was expected to be on the fringes of the American League playoff race , but a 1-9 start and an 8-17 April extinguished most of those hopes . The Blue Jays rebounded with a strong May , but the awful start meant they couldn&amp;rsquo ; t afford any more swoons , and they had just enough of them to curb any serious post-season aspirations . The playoff streak , after two decades outside of them , ended at two seasons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With team management saying it does not intend to embark on a rebuild , the Jays face the difficult task of trying to turn a creaky lineup and some young pitching talent into a competitive roster again . Whatever happens to the Jays on the field in 2018 , it is unlikely to start worse . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;19177;TOOLONG ... title= " Ottawa Senators defenceman Erik Karlsson celebrates a second-round playoff goal against the New York Rangers on May 9 , 2017. " alt= " Ottawa Senators defenceman Erik Karlsson celebrates a second-round playoff goal against the New York Rangers on May 9 , 2017. " data-portal-copyright= " Bruce Bennett " **28;19212;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1374343 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Canada 's back&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the spring of 2016 , our country was in crisis . No Canadian teams made the Stanley Cup playoffs for the first time in more than 50 years . Oh , sure , a team led by Sidney Crosby , who is definitely Canadian , won the Cup , but was there something bigger happening ? Was there something keeping Canadian teams from competing ? Were we smothering teams with our love ? Was it &amp;hellip ; us ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Turns out , nope . Five Canadian teams were back in the playoffs in 2017 , including previous sad sacks in Edmonton and Toronto , now led by two of the best young players in the game . Ottawa went all the way to the conference final . And then Crosby and the Penguins won again . Remind us not to freak out next time Canada has a collective off year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;19242;TOOLONG ... title= " CFL commissioner Randy Ambrosie laughs as he speaks during a press conference in Toronto on July 5. " alt= " CFL commissioner Randy Ambrosie laughs as he speaks during a press conference in Toronto on July 5. " data-portal-copyright= " Frank Gunn " **28;19277;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 76108198 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;At least one thing was familiar&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; There was much change in the Canadian Football League in 2017 . New commissioner Randy Ambrosie arrived and won praise for making quick decisions , including ending full contact practices and talking the Hamilton Tiger-Cats out of the bizarre hiring of ( deservedly ) scandal-plagued former Baylor coach Art Briles. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the on-field stuff was more of the same : the East was a hot mess , the Calgary Stampeders were the class of the West and , once more , the Stampeders were upset in the Grey Cup , this time by the Toronto Argonauts in snowy Ottawa . The year ended with the Argos purchased by MLSE , which means the future of the franchise is as safe as it has ever been . Ambrosie is on some kind of hot streak. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;19307;TOOLONG ... title= " Toronto FC captain Michael Bradley lifts the MLS Cup on Dec. 9. " alt= " Toronto FC captain Michael Bradley lifts the MLS Cup on Dec. 9. " data-portal-copyright= " Vaughn Ridley " **28;19342;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77024412 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Go on you Reds&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Two weeks after that Argonauts championship , Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s soccer team won another trophy for the city , with Toronto FC winning Canada&amp;rsquo ; s first MLS Cup . The tense 2-0 win at BMO Field , in which Seattle goalkeeper Stefan Frei looked like he might force the game to extra time all by himself , was broken open by TFC&amp;rsquo ; s Jozy Altidore in the 65th minute , off passes from the team&amp;rsquo ; s other two offensive stars , Sebastian Giovinco and Victor Vazquez. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It capped a year in which TFC also won the Canadian club championship and the Supporters&amp;rsquo ; Shield as the best team in the regular season , for a first-ever MLS treble . &amp;ldquo ; Plan the parade&amp;rdquo ; has long been such a punchline in Toronto that it was weird when it became an actual thing to do . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;19372;TOOLONG ... title= " Buffalo Bills running back LeSean McCoy kneels during the national anthem before a game against the Denver Broncos on Sept. 24. " alt= " Buffalo Bills running back LeSean McCoy kneels during the national anthem before a game against the Denver Broncos on Sept. 24. " data-portal-copyright= " Bryan Bennett " **28;19407;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 76677291 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Kaep-tain America&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The kneel-during-anthem protests that Colin Kaepernick had started as a protest against racial injustice last season were all but over as a story this fall , with only a few players still kneeling and Kaepernick out of a job . Then Donald Trump went off on NFL players in a speech and the ensuing flap kicked off a debate about an athlete&amp;rsquo ; s right to speak out that touched the NHL and NBA , with players and coaches in that league blasting the U.S. President at every turn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The idea of keeping politics and sports in two separate silos died in 2017 , a development that won&amp;rsquo ; t please certain fans . It also doesn&amp;rsquo ; t please the NFL , which saw TV ratings drop again , although how much of that can be attributed to anthem protests remains a matter of debate . NFL owners responded to the many problems of recent years by extending the contract of commissioner Roger Goodell and paying him many millions more annually than any of the league&amp;rsquo ; s star players receive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was just one more piece of evidence that 2017 was kind of nuts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; &lt;em&gt;Email : &lt;a href= " **29;19437;TOOLONG " **36;19468;TOOLONG Twitter : &lt;a href= " https : **26;19506;TOOLONG " class= " twitter-follow-button " **38;19534;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Scott StinsonFri , 29 Dec 2017 15:00:33 **45;19574;TOOLONG ... it 's cold in Canada now ? 50th anniversary of football 's Ice Bowl might lead you to reconsiderhttp : **30;19621;TOOLONG ... src= " https : **33;19653;TOOLONG ... title= " In this Dec. 31 , 1967 file photo , fans watch the Green Bay Packers play the Dallas Cowboys in the NFL Championship game in Green Bay , Wisc. Tournament organizers told the Associated Press that tomorrow afternoon&amp;rsquo ; s group stage game will indeed be played at New Era Field , home of the NFL Bills , despite forecasted temperatures of -6C to -9C . Canada improved to 2-0 at the under-20 championship last night with a 6-0 shutout of Slovakia . The U.S. is 1-0 and plays Slovakia tonight at 8 p.m . ET . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Former Hurricanes forward Zach Boychuk has been added to Canada&amp;rsquo ; s roster for the remainder of the Spengler Cup . He and his new teammates , each of them a candidate for Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Olympic team , will attempt this afternoon to win the second of their two round robin games in Switzerland . Puck drop against the host club , HC Davos ( 1-0 ) , is at 2:15 p.m . ET . If Canada ( 1-0 ) wins , it will bypass tomorrow&amp;rsquo ; s quarterfinal round and advance straight to the semifinals on Saturday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The Vegas Golden Knights set a record for the longest points streak by a team in its inaugural season with a 4-1 win last night over Anaheim . The Knights have earned a point in 10 straight games ( 9-0-1 ) to better the mark set by the 1993-94 Florida Panthers ( 5-0-4 ) . Goalie Malcolm Subban , who was claimed on waivers from Boston just before the season started , is now 9-2-0 with a 2.18 goals against average . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;19783;TOOLONG ... title= " Vegas Golden Knights goalie Malcolm Subban , right , makes a save against the Anaheim Ducks on Dec. 27. " data-portal-copyright= " Chris Carlson " **28;19818;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77050799 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The NFL playoff scenarios are pretty straightforward for the final week of the regular season . Six of the eight division titles have been wrapped up , three of the four first-round byes are spoken for , and only three teams remain alive that can sneak into a wild-card spot if a team ahead of them falters . The NFL rearranged the schedule this week to make sure all the teams competing for the same slots were playing at the same time to prevent any games from being uncompetitive. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the AFC , New England ( 12-3 ) can claim home-field advantage throughout the playoffs with a win over the Jets , while Pittsburgh ( 12-3 ) needs a win over Cleveland and a Patriots loss to take the No. 1 seed for itself . Both teams play in the 1 p.m . ET time slot . Jacksonville ( 10-5 ) and Kansas City ( 9-6 ) will occupy the No. 3 and 4 seeds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The AFC wild cards are easy if the current holders , Baltimore ( 9-6 ) and Tenneessee ( 8-7 ) , win their games . The L.A. Chargers ( 8-7 ) and Buffalo ( 8-7 ) need to win and need various combinations of losses or ties from the other teams in the wild-card chase . All these games will be played at 4:25 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;19848;TOOLONG ... title= " New England Patriots tight end Rob Gronkowski ( right ) scores a touchdown against the Buffalo Bills on Dec. 24. " alt= " New England Patriots tight end Rob Gronkowski ( right ) scores a touchdown against the Buffalo Bills on Dec. 24. " data-portal-copyright= " Maddie Meyer " **28;19883;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77050801 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the NFC , Philadelphia ( 13-2 ) is locked in as the top seed , but Minnesota ( 12-3 ) needs to beat Chicago to clinch No. 2 ( although the Vikings can lose and keep the No. 2 seed if the Rams lose and Carolina and New Orleans both win ) . Carolina ( 11-4 ) is the only other team that can occupy the No. 2 seed , which will happen if it beats Atlanta and New Orleans , Minnesota and the Rams all lose . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Rams will host a wild-card round game regardless of their outcome on Sunday and the Panthers have at least a wild-card spot locked up regardless of their game . The Saints are the AFC South champion with a win ; the Panthers can grab that title with a win and a Saints loss . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Atlanta can still claim a wild card if it wins or Seattle loses , while the Seahawks take the spot with a win and a Falcons loss . See the TV listings below for the full slate of games available on CTV and TSN. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; This Sunday &amp;mdash ; New Year&amp;rsquo ; s Eve &amp;mdash ; is the 50th anniversary of the Ice Bowl , Green Bay&amp;rsquo ; s 21-17 home victory over Dallas in an NFL Championship Game played with a windchill of -44C . Packers QB Bart Starr scored the winning touchdown on a one-yard sneak with 13 seconds left , validating Vince Lombardi&amp;rsquo ; s call to eschew a potential game-tying field goal attempt . &amp;ldquo ; You can&amp;rsquo ; t say I&amp;rsquo ; m always without compassion , &amp;rdquo ; Lombardi said with a smile after the game . " data-portal-copyright= " Chris Young " **28;22842;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77048999 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Early in the tenure of Raptors coach Dwane Casey , he used to tell reporters that his goal for team defence was to hold opponents below 43 per cent shooting . As it turned out , that was a worthy goal in his first season , 2011-12 , when the Raptors held opponents to 43.5 per cent shooting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The closest they&amp;rsquo ; ve come since then is 44.4 per cent in 2015-16 , although Casey&amp;rsquo ; s target may have changed as his teams became offensive powerhouses and as the league evolved into trying to shoot as much as it could from the paint and from beyond the three-point line . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last night&amp;rsquo ; s dismal 98-93 loss in Dallas was beyond weird for the number of times the teams traded scoring runs , alternating wildly between being hot and cold . The Mavericks , in fact , shot only 42.6 per cent . The Raptors could only manage to shoot 33.7 per cent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below are a couple of charts showing how the Raptors&amp;rsquo ; offensive and defensive shooting percentages have evolved over Casey&amp;rsquo ; s seven seasons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;22872;TOOLONG ... class= " aligncenter size-full wp-image-77048973 " src= " https : **33;22907;TOOLONG ... alt= " " width= " 640 " height= " 600 " srcset= " https : **31;22942;TOOLONG ... 640w , https : **39;22975;TOOLONG ... 150w , https : **39;23016;TOOLONG ... 300w " sizes= " ( max-width : 640px ) 100vw , 640px " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;23057;TOOLONG ... class= " aligncenter size-full wp-image-77048972 " src= " https : **33;23092;TOOLONG ... alt= " " width= " 640 " height= " 600 " srcset= " https : **31;23127;TOOLONG ... 640w , https : **39;23160;TOOLONG ... 150w , https : **39;23201;TOOLONG ... 300w " sizes= " ( max-width : 640px ) 100vw , 640px " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s 47.4 per cent shooting this season ranks seventh in the league ; last season&amp;rsquo ; s 46.4 mark was 11th . On defence , oddly , they rank 25th in both seasons with identical 44.9 marks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The box plots above show the range of the Raptors per-game shooting percentages , with the box representing the range between the 25th and 75th percentile and the line in the middle of the box showing the median . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although the Raptors haven&amp;rsquo ; t moved the needle on their overall defensive percentage in the last two seasons , the spread between the offensive and defensive medians is at its highest level in Casey&amp;rsquo ; s seven seasons . Last season , the spread was 2.0 percentage points ( 46.65 offence vs. 44.65 defence ) and this season the spread is 1.6 percentage points ( 46.70 vs. 45.10 ) , even as the team has tried to evolve into taking more three-point shots . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Photo of the day&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; As 2017 draws to an end , here are 12 momentous snapshots from the past year &amp;mdash ; one for each month . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;At **27;23242;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Don Cherry was worried he was getting soft . Another franchise , believed to be Sacramento , is expected to be announced in the coming weeks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The &lt;a href= " https : **33;24578;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;World Chess Championship&lt;/a&gt; got some perhaps unexpected attention yesterday when Twitter users started sharing one of its graphic designs for the 2018 tournament &amp;mdash ; emphasis on graphic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;24613;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24648;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Telegraph went so far as to call it &amp;ldquo ; Pawnographic&amp;rdquo ; in a headline . World Chess doubled down later in the day by offering limited edition prints . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Let&amp;rsquo ; s circle back to Sunday , when NFL referee Gene Steratore famously used a card from his pocket to decide whether the Cowboys had been successful on a fourth-down play with just under five minutes left in the fourth quarter with the score tied 17-17 against the Raiders . &lt;a href= " http : //www.nfl.com/m/share ? p=%2Fvideos% ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The game hung in the balance . &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After Cowboys quarterback Dak Prescott ran a quarterback sneak , the ball was spotted with the tip abutting the 40-yard line . After the chains came out to measure , which did not appear to solve the mystery , Steratore pulled a card out of his shirt pocket and used it in some manner against the tip of the football . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As Ben Austro explained on his website &lt;a href= " http : **34;24678;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;24714;TOOLONG ... the whole card thing was sort of meaningless because the officiating crew knows exactly what they&amp;rsquo ; re looking at . On first downs , to guard against these kinds of dramatic snafus , the ball is always placed touching a yard-line marker . The crew would have known this , and really didn&amp;rsquo ; t need the chains to decide if it was a first down . They would know by looking down from above as to whether the ball was on or over the 40-yard line marker . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Steratore may have been using the card to see whether there was any 40-yard-line paint under the card when he placed it against the tip of the ball . The chains , it would appear , were irrelevant to the whole exercise . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As happens after any controversial call by an official , Steratore was asked to meet with a pool reporter chosen from among the beat reporters in attendance . Two Raiders beat reporters , &lt;a href= " https : **33;24748;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Michael Gehlken&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href= " https : **33;24783;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vic Tafur&lt;/a&gt; , tweeted the transcript of Steratore&amp;rsquo ; s remarks after the game . It&amp;rsquo ; s not clear who the pool reporter was . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;24818;TOOLONG ... title= " Dallas Cowboys quarterback Dak Prescott scores a touchdown against the Oakland Raiders on Dec. 17. " alt= " Dallas Cowboys quarterback Dak Prescott scores a touchdown against the Oakland Raiders on Dec. 17. " data-portal-copyright= " Lachlan Cunningham " **28;24853;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77038893 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below are Steratore&amp;rsquo ; s comments , along with the questions posed by the pool reporter . Steratore REALLY wanted to be clear that the card had very little to do with his decision . The reporter couldn&amp;rsquo ; t quite get Steratore to explain how he used the card to &amp;ldquo ; reaffirm&amp;rdquo ; the call , which would have helped stop the launch of a thousand Raiders conspiracy theories . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;24883;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; Why did you use the index card ? &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Gene Steratore : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Didn&amp; ... use the card to make the final decision . The final decision was made visually . The card was used nothing more than a reaffirmation of what was visually done . My decision was visually done based on the look from the pole . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;24910;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; How did it reaffirm ? &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Steratore : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp; ... was already finished . The ball was touching the pole . I put the card in there and as soon as it touched , it was nothing more than a reaffirmation . The decision was made based on my visual from the top looking down and the ball touching the front of the pole . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;24937;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; So the card was used for what purpose ? &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Steratore : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp; ... was just for reaffirmation , but the decision was made based on my visual , looking at the ball touching the pole . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;24964;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; It reaffirmed it how ? &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Steratore : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp; ... decision was made based on my visual look that the ball was touching the pole . The card did nothing more than reaffirm . The judgement was not made by the card itself . It was made by my visual looking at the football as it relates to the line and the pole . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;24991;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; How did it reaffirm your call ? &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Steratore : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp; ... call was made based on my visual looking at the football and the front of the line and the pole . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;25018;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; Have you used the card before or how did you think of using that ? &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Steratore : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp; ... maybe been done at some point in someone&amp;rsquo ; s career , but I didn&amp;rsquo ; t use the card for my decision . I used my visual looking at the ball reaching the pole . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For today&amp;rsquo ; s analysis , we broke down the 215 words in Steratore&amp;rsquo ; s comments and found that the same 11 words ( or iterations of the same word ) covered 33 per cent of his utterances . " data-portal-copyright= " Streeter Lecka " **28;26035;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 77034239 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nutritional analysis&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; During Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s Packers-Panthers telecast , FOX&amp;rsquo ; s Joe Buck mentioned that Carolina quarterback Cam Newton was only the third quarterback in NFL history to pass for 150 touchdowns and run for 4,000 yards . It seemed like it might be interesting to see what a plot of that would look like . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26065;TOOLONG ... class= " aligncenter size-full wp-image-77033802 " src= " https : **33;26100;TOOLONG ... alt= " " width= " 640 " height= " 515 " srcset= " https : **31;26135;TOOLONG ... 640w , https : **39;26168;TOOLONG ... 150w , https : **39;26209;TOOLONG ... 300w " sizes= " ( max-width : 640px ) 100vw , 640px " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The three relevant players are noted : Newton &amp;mdash ; who threw four touchdown passes and ran 14 times for 58 yards in the 31-24 victory over Green Bay &amp;mdash ; Randall Cunningham and Steve Young . While the achievement seems noteworthy , in that so few players have reached that combination , the visualization of the data shows it might be much more interesting when someone with running ability also moves into an elite passing echelon. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below is the same chart with the last eight Super Bowl-winning quarterbacks marked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26250;TOOLONG ... class= " aligncenter size-full wp-image-77033801 " src= " https : **33;26285;TOOLONG ... alt= " " width= " 640 " height= " 515 " srcset= " https : **31;26320;TOOLONG ... 640w , https : **39;26353;TOOLONG ... 150w , https : **39;26394;TOOLONG ... 300w " sizes= " ( max-width : 640px ) 100vw , 640px " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among Cunningham , Newton and Young , only Young has a Super Bowl ring and only one of his three rings came when he was the starter . Newton has made a Super Bowl appearance , losing to Peyton Manning and the Broncos at the end of the 2015 season . Cunningham never got to the title game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Running quarterbacks remain crowd-pleasers , but they don&amp;rsquo ; t appear to be the ones who get the job done in January and February . Newton has a chance to break that mould if he can win more than one title . Russell Wilson needs fewer than 800 rushing yards to join the 150-4,000 club , and he also has a Super Bowl ring and two appearances . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not exactly fair to compare Newton unfavourably to the last eight Super Bowl-winning quarterbacks , since five of them probably will be Hall of Famers and Eli might sneak into Canton as well . That&amp;rsquo ; s two-thirds of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s top scoring line gone ; Bo Horvat fractured his foot last Tuesday and will be gone for six weeks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The Columbus Blue Jackets and Toronto Maple Leafs became the ninth and 10th teams in NHL history to score in the first minute of a game and hold on for a 1-0 win in regulation time , the Elias Sports Bureau reports via the NHL . Columbus did it Saturday against Arizona , then Toronto did it Sunday against Edmonton . Teams that score in the first minute of the game are 22-9-2 this season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;27002;TOOLONG ... title= " Toronto FC coach Greg Vanney , rear left to right , and players Drew Moor , Sebastian Giovinco , Michael Bradley , Jozy Altidore and Jonathan Osorio take part in the ceremonial puck drop before a Toronto Maple Leafs-Edmonton Oilers game on Dec. 10. The team confirmed reports that Wentz tore his ACL on an awkward tackle in the third quarter of their 43-35 win over the Los Angeles Rams. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Cristiano Ronaldo will match wits with Neymar in the next stage of the Champions League , the competition&amp;rsquo ; s round-of-16 draw determined today . Paris Saint Germain , the powerful French club Neymar joined in August , scored 25 goals and allowed only four through six games in the group stage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other prominent pairings dictated by the draw include Tottenham vs .
